    itemIds: request.itemIds,
    characterId: request.characterId,
    membershipType: request.membershipType,
  }, accessToken);
}

export interface InsertPlugRequest {
  itemInstanceId: string;
  socketIndex: number;
  plugItemHash: number;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

export async function insertSocketPlug(request: InsertPlugRequest, accessToken: string): Promise<any> {
  return bungiePost('/Platform/Destiny2/Actions/Items/InsertSocketPlugFree/', {
    itemInstanceId: request.itemInstanceId,
    plug: {
      socketIndex: request.socketIndex,
      socketArrayType: 0,
      plugItemHash: request.plugItemHash,
    },
    characterId: request.characterId,
    membershipType: request.membershipType,
  }, accessToken);
}

export interface EquipLoadoutRequest {
  loadoutIndex: number;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

export async function equipLoadout(request: EquipLoadoutRequest, accessToken: string): Promise<any> {
  return bungiePost('/Platform/Destiny2/Actions/Loadouts/EquipLoadout/', {
    loadoutIndex: request.loadoutIndex,
    characterId: request.characterId,
    membershipType: request.membershipType,
  }, accessToken);
}

export interface SnapshotLoadoutRequest {
  loadoutIndex: number;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
  nameHash?: number;
  colorHash?: number;
  iconHash?: number;
}

export async function snapshotLoadout(request: SnapshotLoadoutRequest, accessToken: string): Promise<any> {
  const body: any = {
    loadoutIndex: request.loadoutIndex,
    characterId: request.characterId,
    membershipType: request.membershipType,
  };
  if (request.nameHash) body.nameHash = request.nameHash;
  if (request.colorHash) body.colorHash = request.colorHash;
  if (request.iconHash) body.iconHash = request.iconHash;

  return bungiePost('/Platform/Destiny2/Actions/Loadouts/SnapshotLoadout/', body, accessToken);
}

export interface ClearLoadoutRequest {
  loadoutIndex: number;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

export async function clearLoadout(request: ClearLoadoutRequest, accessToken: string): Promise<any> {
  return bungiePost('/Platform/Destiny2/Actions/Loadouts/ClearLoadout/', {
    loadoutIndex: request.loadoutIndex,
    characterId: request.characterId,
    membershipType: request.membershipType,
  }, accessToken);
}

export async function fetchMembershipsRaw(accessToken: string) {
  return bungieGet('/Platform/User/GetMembershipsForCurrentUser/', accessToken);
}

export interface BungieNewsArticle {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  link: string;
  image: string;
  date: string;
  description: string;
  contentType: string;
}

const newsCacheMap = new Map<string, { articles: BungieNewsArticle[]; fetchedAt: number }>();
const NEWS_CACHE_TTL = 10 * 60 * 1000;

export async function fetchBungieNews(language: string = 'en'): Promise<BungieNewsArticle[]> {
  const locale = language === 'de' ? 'de' : language === 'fr' ? 'fr' : 'en';
  const cached = newsCacheMap.get(locale);
  if (cached && Date.now() - cached.fetchedAt < NEWS_CACHE_TTL) {
    return cached.articles;
  }

  const res = await bungieGet(`/Platform/Content/Rss/NewsArticles/0/?categoryfilter=&includebody=false&newsonly=true`);
  const data = typeof res === 'string' ? JSON.parse(res) : res;

  if (!data?.Response?.NewsArticles) {
    return [];
  }

  const articles: BungieNewsArticle[] = data.Response.NewsArticles
    .slice(0, 20)
    .map((article: any) => ({
      title: article.Title || '',
      subtitle: article.Description || article.OptionalMobileTitle || '',
      link: article.Link ? `https://www.bungie.net${article.Link}` : '',
      image: article.ImagePath ? (article.ImagePath.startsWith('http') ? article.ImagePath : `https://www.bungie.net${article.ImagePath}`) : '',
      date: article.PubDate || article.CreationDate || '',
      description: article.Description || article.HtmlContent || '',
      contentType: detectContentType(article.Title || '', article.Link || ''),
    }));

  newsCacheMap.set(locale, { articles, fetchedAt: Date.now() });
  return articles;
}

function detectContentType(title: string, link: string): string {
  const lower = title.toLowerCase();
  if (lower.includes('this week in destiny') || lower.includes('this week at bungie') || lower.includes('twid') || lower.includes('twab')) return 'twid';
  if (lower.includes('patch') || lower.includes('hotfix') || lower.includes('update')) return 'patch';
  if (lower.includes('maintenance') || lower.includes('downtime')) return 'maintenance';
  if (lower.includes('season') || lower.includes('expansion') || lower.includes('reveal')) return 'announcement';
  return 'news';
}

const ACTIVITY_MODE_MAP: Record<string, number> = {
  all: 0,
  raid: 4,
  dungeon: 82,
  nightfall: 46,
  crucible: 5,
  gambit: 63,
  trials: 84,
  ironbanner: 43,
  strike: 18,
};

export interface ActivityHistoryEntry {
  instanceId: string;
  activityHash: number;
  activityName: string;
  activityIcon: string;
  mode: string;
  modeType: number;
  date: string;
  duration: number;
  completed: boolean;
  kills: number;
  deaths: number;
  assists: number;
  kd: number;
  teamScore: number;
  playerCount: number;
  isFlawless: boolean;
}

export async function fetchActivityHistory(
  accessToken: string,
  membershipType: string,
  membershipId: string,
  characterId: string,
  mode: string = 'raid',
  count: number = 25,
  page: number = 0
): Promise<ActivityHistoryEntry[]> {
  const modeNum = ACTIVITY_MODE_MAP[mode] ?? 4;
  const manifest = await fetchManifest();

  const data = await bungieGet(
    `/Platform/Destiny2/${membershipType}/Account/${membershipId}/Character/${characterId}/Stats/Activities/?mode=${modeNum}&count=${count}&page=${page}`,
    accessToken
  );

  const activities = data?.Response?.activities;
  if (!activities || !Array.isArray(activities)) {
    return [];
  }

  return activities.map((a: any) => {
    const vals = a.values || {};
    const activityHash = a.activityDetails?.referenceId;
    const actDef = activityHash ? manifest.activities[String(activityHash)] : null;
    const deaths = vals.deaths?.basic?.value ?? 0;
    const kills = vals.kills?.basic?.value ?? 0;

    return {
      instanceId: a.activityDetails?.instanceId || '',
      activityHash: activityHash || 0,
      activityName: actDef?.name || 'Unknown Activity',
      activityIcon: actDef?.icon ? `https://www.bungie.net${actDef.icon}` : '',
      mode,
      modeType: a.activityDetails?.mode || modeNum,
      date: a.period || '',
      duration: vals.activityDurationSeconds?.basic?.value ?? 0,
      completed: (vals.completed?.basic?.value ?? 0) === 1,
      kills,
      deaths,
      assists: vals.assists?.basic?.value ?? 0,
      kd: deaths > 0 ? parseFloat((kills / deaths).toFixed(2)) : kills,
      teamScore: vals.teamScore?.basic?.value ?? 0,
      playerCount: vals.playerCount?.basic?.value ?? 0,
      isFlawless: deaths === 0 && (vals.completed?.basic?.value ?? 0) === 1,
    };
  });
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: server/ai.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import OpenAI from "openai";
import { z } from "zod";

const openai = new OpenAI({
  apiKey: process.env.AI_INTEGRATIONS_OPENAI_API_KEY,
  baseURL: process.env.AI_INTEGRATIONS_OPENAI_BASE_URL,
});

function getLangInstruction(language: string): string {
  return language === 'de'
    ? 'Antworte auf Deutsch.'
    : language === 'fr'
    ? 'Réponds en français.'
    : 'Respond in English.';
}

async function callAI<T>(prompt: string, schema: z.ZodSchema<T>): Promise<T> {
  const response = await openai.chat.completions.create({
    model: "gpt-4o",
    messages: [{ role: "user", content: prompt }],
    response_format: { type: "json_object" },
    max_completion_tokens: 8192,
  });

  const content = response.choices[0]?.message?.content;
  if (!content) {
    throw new Error('No response from AI');
  }

  const raw = JSON.parse(content);
  return schema.parse(raw);
}

export const characterAnalysisInputSchema = z.object({
  className: z.string().min(1),
  raceName: z.string().default('Unknown'),
  light: z.number().default(0),
  stats: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), value: z.number() })).default([]),
  equippedItems: z.array(z.object({
    slot: z.string(),
    name: z.string(),
    type: z.string().default(''),
    rarity: z.string().default(''),
    power: z.number().default(0),
    perks: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), isActive: z.boolean() })).default([]),
    mods: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string() })).default([]),
  })).default([]),
  subclass: z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    sockets: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), category: z.string() })).default([]),
  }).nullable().default(null),
  artifact: z.object({
    powerBonus: z.number(),
    activeMods: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
  }).nullable().default(null),
});

export type CharacterAnalysisInput = z.infer<typeof characterAnalysisInputSchema>;

const playstyleAnalysisSchema = z.object({
  playstyle: z.string(),
  summary: z.string(),
  rating: z.number(),
  strengths: z.array(z.string()),
  weaknesses: z.array(z.string()),
  synergies: z.array(z.string()),
  tips: z.array(z.string()),
});

export type PlaystyleAnalysis = z.infer<typeof playstyleAnalysisSchema>;

function buildPlaystylePrompt(data: CharacterAnalysisInput, language: string): string {
  const statsStr = data.stats.map(s => `${s.name}: ${s.value}`).join(', ');

  const weaponsStr = data.equippedItems
    .filter(i => ['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'].includes(i.slot))
    .map(w => {
      const activePerks = w.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name).join(', ');
      const mods = w.mods.map(m => m.name).join(', ');
      return `${w.slot}: ${w.name} (${w.type}, ${w.rarity}) [Perks: ${activePerks || 'none'}] [Mods: ${mods || 'none'}]`;
    }).join('\n');

  const armorStr = data.equippedItems
    .filter(i => ['helmet', 'arms', 'chest', 'legs', 'class'].includes(i.slot))
    .map(a => {
      const mods = a.mods.map(m => m.name).join(', ');
      return `${a.slot}: ${a.name} (${a.rarity}) [Mods: ${mods || 'none'}]`;
    }).join('\n');

  const subclassStr = data.subclass
    ? `Subclass: ${data.subclass.name}\nAbilities: ${data.subclass.sockets.map(s => `${s.category}: ${s.name}`).join(', ')}`
    : 'Subclass: Unknown';

  const artifactStr = data.artifact
    ? `Artifact Power Bonus: +${data.artifact.powerBonus}\nActive Artifact Mods: ${data.artifact.activeMods.join(', ') || 'none'}`
    : '';

  return `You are an expert Destiny 2 build analyst and PvE/PvP strategist. Analyze this Guardian's build and provide a detailed playstyle analysis.

${getLangInstruction(language)}

CHARACTER:
Class: ${data.className} (${data.raceName})
Power Level: ${data.light}

STATS:
${statsStr}

WEAPONS:
${weaponsStr}

ARMOR:
${armorStr}

${subclassStr}

${artifactStr}

Analyze this build and return a JSON object with exactly these fields:
{
  "playstyle": "A short, punchy 2-4 word playstyle label (e.g. 'Aggressiver Nahkämpfer', 'Precision Marksman', 'Support Healer')",
  "summary": "A 2-3 sentence summary of how this build plays and what it excels at",
  "rating": <number 1-10 rating of the overall build cohesion and effectiveness>,
  "strengths": ["strength 1", "strength 2", "strength 3"],
  "weaknesses": ["weakness 1", "weakness 2"],
  "synergies": ["Describe a specific synergy between 2+ parts of the build", "Another synergy"],
  "tips": ["Specific actionable improvement tip 1", "Tip 2", "Tip 3"]
}

Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown, no code fences, no extra text.`;
}

export async function analyzePlaystyle(
  data: CharacterAnalysisInput,
  language: string = 'de'
): Promise<PlaystyleAnalysis> {
  const prompt = buildPlaystylePrompt(data, language);
  const parsed = await callAI(prompt, playstyleAnalysisSchema);
  parsed.rating = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.round(parsed.rating)));
  return parsed;
}

const buildRecommendationSchema = z.object({
  activity: z.string(),
  recommendedWeapons: z.array(z.object({
    slot: z.string(),
    name: z.string(),
    reason: z.string(),
  })),
  recommendedArmor: z.array(z.object({
    slot: z.string(),
    name: z.string(),
    reason: z.string(),
  })),
  recommendedSubclass: z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    aspects: z.array(z.string()),
    fragments: z.array(z.string()),
  }),
  statPriorities: z.array(z.string()),
  strategy: z.string(),
});

export type BuildRecommendation = z.infer<typeof buildRecommendationSchema>;

export async function generateBuildRecommendation(
  data: BuildRecommendationInput,
  language: string = 'de'
): Promise<BuildRecommendation> {
  const weaponsList = data.availableWeapons
    .slice(0, 60)
    .map(w => `${w.slot}: ${w.name} (${w.type}, ${w.rarity}, ${w.power}) [Perks: ${w.perks.join(', ') || 'none'}]`)
    .join('\n');

  const armorList = data.availableArmor
    .slice(0, 30)
    .map(a => `${a.slot}: ${a.name} (${a.rarity})`)
    .join('\n');

  const subclassStr = data.subclass
    ? `Current Subclass: ${data.subclass.name}\nAbilities: ${data.subclass.sockets.map(s => `${s.category}: ${s.name}`).join(', ')}`
    : 'Subclass: Not set';

  const statsStr = data.stats.map(s => `${s.name}: ${s.value}`).join(', ');

  const activityDescriptions: Record<string, string> = {
    raid: 'Raid (6-player endgame PvE, boss DPS, ad clear, survivability)',
    nightfall: 'Grandmaster Nightfall (high-level PvE, match game, champions)',
    pvp: 'Crucible PvP (Trials of Osiris / Competitive)',
    gambit: 'Gambit (hybrid PvE/PvP, invading, boss melt)',
    dungeon: 'Dungeon (3-player endgame PvE, solo-friendly)',
  };

  const prompt = `You are an expert Destiny 2 build crafter. Recommend the BEST loadout for ${activityDescriptions[data.activity] || data.activity} using ONLY the weapons and armor the player actually owns. Pick from the available items below.

${getLangInstruction(language)}

CLASS: ${data.className}
POWER: ${data.light}
STATS: ${statsStr}

${subclassStr}

AVAILABLE WEAPONS:
${weaponsList || 'No weapons available'}

AVAILABLE ARMOR:
${armorList || 'No armor available'}

Return a JSON object with exactly these fields:
{
  "activity": "${data.activity}",
  "recommendedWeapons": [{"slot": "kinetic|energy|power", "name": "exact weapon name from list", "reason": "why this weapon is great for this activity"}],
  "recommendedArmor": [{"slot": "helmet|arms|chest|legs|class", "name": "exact armor name from list", "reason": "why this armor piece"}],
  "recommendedSubclass": {"name": "subclass name", "aspects": ["aspect1", "aspect2"], "fragments": ["fragment1", "fragment2", "fragment3"]},
  "statPriorities": ["Most important stat for this activity", "Second", "Third"],
  "strategy": "2-3 sentence strategy for how to play this build in the activity"
}

IMPORTANT: Only recommend items that appear in the AVAILABLE lists above. Return ONLY valid JSON.`;

  return callAI(prompt, buildRecommendationSchema);
}

});

    name: z.string(),

    explanation: z.string(),

export async function evaluateWeaponRoll(
  data: WeaponRollInput,
  language: string = 'de'
): Promise<WeaponRollEvaluation> {
  const perksStr = data.perks
    .map(p => `${p.name}${p.isActive ? ' (active)' : ''} [column ${p.column}]`)
    .join('\n');

  const modsStr = data.mods.map(m => m.name).join(', ') || 'none';

  const prompt = `You are an expert Destiny 2 weapon analyst. Evaluate this weapon roll in detail.

${getLangInstruction(language)}

WEAPON: ${data.name}
Type: ${data.type}
Slot: ${data.slot}
Rarity: ${data.rarity}
Power: ${data.power}
Masterwork: ${data.masterwork || 'none'}

PERKS:
${perksStr || 'No perks'}

MODS: ${modsStr}

Analyze each perk individually and the overall roll quality. Consider PvE and PvP viability.

Return a JSON object with exactly these fields:
{
  "verdict": "god_roll" | "great" | "good" | "average" | "shard",
  "score": <number 1-10>,
  "perkBreakdown": [{"name": "perk name", "rating": "s|a|b|c|f", "explanation": "why this perk is good/bad for this weapon"}],
  "idealPerks": ["ideal perk 1 for this weapon", "ideal perk 2"],
  "summary": "2-3 sentence summary of this roll's quality and best use case"
}

Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown, no code fences, no extra text.`;

  const parsed = await callAI(prompt, weaponRollSchema);
  parsed.score = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.round(parsed.score)));
  return parsed;
}

export const triumphAdvisorInputSchema = z.object({
  incompleteRecords: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    description: z.string().default(''),
    objectives: z.array(z.object({
      description: z.string(),
      progress: z.number().default(0),
      completionValue: z.number().default(1),
      complete: z.boolean().default(false),
    })).default([]),
  })).default([]),
  className: z.string().default(''),
  equippedItems: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    type: z.string().default(''),
    slot: z.string().default(''),
  })).default([]),
});

export type TriumphAdvisorInput = z.infer<typeof triumphAdvisorInputSchema>;

const triumphAdvisorSchema = z.object({
  prioritizedTriumphs: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    reason: z.string(),
    estimatedTime: z.string(),
    tips: z.array(z.string()),
  })),
  generalAdvice: z.string(),
});

export type TriumphAdvisorResult = z.infer<typeof triumphAdvisorSchema>;

export async function suggestTriumphPath(
  data: TriumphAdvisorInput,
  language: string = 'de'
): Promise<TriumphAdvisorResult> {
  const recordsStr = data.incompleteRecords
    .slice(0, 30)
    .map(r => {
      const objStr = r.objectives
        .map(o => `  - ${o.description}: ${o.progress}/${o.completionValue}${o.complete ? ' (DONE)' : ''}`)
        .join('\n');
      return `${r.name}: ${r.description}\n${objStr}`;
    }).join('\n\n');

  const equippedStr = data.equippedItems
    .map(i => `${i.slot}: ${i.name} (${i.type})`)
    .join('\n');

  const prompt = `You are a Destiny 2 triumph hunting expert. Analyze the player's incomplete triumphs and recommend the most efficient path to complete them.

${getLangInstruction(language)}

CLASS: ${data.className}

CURRENTLY EQUIPPED:
${equippedStr || 'Unknown'}

INCOMPLETE TRIUMPHS (with progress):
${recordsStr || 'No triumphs available'}

Prioritize triumphs that are closest to completion and can be done with the player's current setup. Consider which triumphs can be worked on simultaneously.

Return a JSON object with exactly these fields:
{
  "prioritizedTriumphs": [
    {"name": "triumph name", "reason": "why prioritize this one", "estimatedTime": "~30 min", "tips": ["specific tip 1", "tip 2"]}
  ],
  "generalAdvice": "2-3 sentences of general triumph hunting strategy for this player"
}

Return 5-8 prioritized triumphs. Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown, no code fences, no extra text.`;

  return callAI(prompt, triumphAdvisorSchema);
}

export const synergyInputSchema = z.object({
  className: z.string().min(1),
  stats: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), value: z.number() })).default([]),
  equippedItems: z.array(z.object({
    slot: z.string(),
    name: z.string(),
    type: z.string().default(''),
    rarity: z.string().default(''),
    perks: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), isActive: z.boolean() })).default([]),
    mods: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string() })).default([]),
  })).default([]),
  subclass: z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    element: z.string().default(''),
    sockets: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), category: z.string() })).default([]),
  }).nullable().default(null),
  armorMods: z.array(z.object({
    slot: z.string(),
    mods: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string() })).default([]),
  })).default([]),
});

export type SynergyInput = z.infer<typeof synergyInputSchema>;

const synergyAnalysisSchema = z.object({
  overallRating: z.number(),
  synergies: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    strength: z.enum(['strong', 'moderate', 'weak']),
    items: z.array(z.string()),
    perks: z.array(z.string()),
    explanation: z.string(),
  })),
  antiSynergies: z.array(z.object({
    issue: z.string(),
    items: z.array(z.string()),
    explanation: z.string(),
    fix: z.string(),
  })),
  suggestions: z.array(z.object({
    current: z.string(),
    recommended: z.string(),
    reason: z.string(),
  })),
  summary: z.string(),
});

export type SynergyAnalysis = z.infer<typeof synergyAnalysisSchema>;

export async function analyzeSynergies(
  data: SynergyInput,
  language: string = 'de'
): Promise<SynergyAnalysis> {
  const weaponsStr = data.equippedItems
    .filter(i => ['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'].includes(i.slot))
    .map(w => {
      const activePerks = w.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name).join(', ');
      const mods = w.mods.map(m => m.name).join(', ');
      return `${w.slot}: ${w.name} (${w.type}, ${w.rarity}) [Perks: ${activePerks || 'none'}] [Mods: ${mods || 'none'}]`;
    }).join('\n');

  const armorStr = data.equippedItems
    .filter(i => ['helmet', 'arms', 'chest', 'legs', 'class'].includes(i.slot))
    .map(a => {
      const perks = a.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name).join(', ');
      const mods = a.mods.map(m => m.name).join(', ');
      return `${a.slot}: ${a.name} (${a.rarity}) [Perks: ${perks || 'none'}] [Mods: ${mods || 'none'}]`;
    }).join('\n');

  const statsStr = data.stats.map(s => `${s.name}: ${s.value}`).join(', ');

  const subclassStr = data.subclass
    ? `Subclass: ${data.subclass.name} (${data.subclass.element})\nAbilities/Fragments: ${data.subclass.sockets.map(s => `${s.category}: ${s.name}`).join(', ')}`
    : 'Subclass: Unknown';

  const prompt = `You are an expert Destiny 2 build theorycrafting specialist focused on PERK AND MOD SYNERGIES. Your job is to find every meaningful interaction between the equipped gear's perks, mods, subclass abilities, and armor stats.

${getLangInstruction(language)}

CLASS: ${data.className}

STATS:
${statsStr}

WEAPONS:
${weaponsStr}

ARMOR (with perks and mods):
${armorStr}

${subclassStr}

IMPORTANT: Look for CROSS-ITEM synergies specifically:
- Armor perks that boost weapon effectiveness (e.g., "Ashes to Assets" on helmet + grenade builds = super energy)
- Weapon perks that synergize with subclass abilities (e.g., "Incandescent" + Solar subclass fragments)
- Mod combinations across armor pieces that create loops (e.g., "Font of Might" + matching element weapons)
- Stat thresholds that enable build mechanics (e.g., 100 Discipline + grenade-focused mods)
- Weapon perk combos that chain together (e.g., "Demolitionist" + "Adrenaline Junkie")
- Armor charge mechanics (collecting/spending orbs, elemental wells)
- Anti-synergies where perks conflict or are wasted

Return a JSON object with exactly these fields:
{
  "overallRating": <number 1-10 rating of how well the equipment synergizes>,
  "synergies": [
    {
      "name": "Short synergy combo name (2-5 words)",
      "strength": "strong" | "moderate" | "weak",
      "items": ["Item/slot name 1", "Item/slot name 2"],
      "perks": ["Perk/mod name 1", "Perk/mod name 2"],
      "explanation": "Detailed explanation of HOW these interact and WHY it's effective"
    }
  ],
  "antiSynergies": [
    {
      "issue": "Short description of the conflict",
      "items": ["Item 1", "Item 2"],
      "explanation": "Why these don't work well together",
      "fix": "What to swap to fix this"
    }
  ],
  "suggestions": [
    {
      "current": "Current perk/mod name",
      "recommended": "Recommended replacement",
      "reason": "Why this swap improves synergy"
    }
  ],
  "summary": "2-3 sentence overview of the build's synergy quality and identity"
}

Find 4-8 synergies, 0-3 anti-synergies, and 2-4 suggestions. Be specific about Destiny 2 game mechanics. Return ONLY valid JSON.`;

  const result = await callAI(prompt, synergyAnalysisSchema);
  result.overallRating = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.round(result.overallRating)));
  return result;
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: server/storage.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { db } from "./db";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  savedLoadouts,
  sessions,
  aiUsage,
  userPremium,
  type InsertLoadout,
  type SavedLoadout,
  type Session,
  type InsertSession,
  type AiUsage,
  type UserPremium,
} from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, lt, and } from "drizzle-orm";

export interface IStorage {
  getLoadouts(bungieMembershipId: string): Promise<SavedLoadout[]>;
  getLoadoutByShareCode(shareCode: string): Promise<SavedLoadout | undefined>;
  createLoadout(loadout: InsertLoadout): Promise<SavedLoadout>;
  deleteLoadout(id: number): Promise<boolean>;

  createSession(session: InsertSession): Promise<Session>;
  getSession(id: string): Promise<Session | undefined>;
  updateSessionTokens(id: string, accessToken: string, refreshToken: string | null, tokenExpiresAt: Date): Promise<void>;
  deleteSession(id: string): Promise<void>;
  deleteExpiredSessions(): Promise<void>;

  getAiUsage(bungieMembershipId: string, date: string): Promise<AiUsage | undefined>;
  incrementAiUsage(bungieMembershipId: string, date: string): Promise<AiUsage>;

  getPremiumStatus(bungieMembershipId: string): Promise<UserPremium | undefined>;
  setPremiumStatus(bungieMembershipId: string, isPremium: boolean, premiumUntil?: Date): Promise<UserPremium>;
}

export class DatabaseStorage implements IStorage {
  async getLoadouts(bungieMembershipId: string): Promise<SavedLoadout[]> {
    return await db.select().from(savedLoadouts).where(eq(savedLoadouts.bungieMembershipId, bungieMembershipId));
  }

  async getLoadoutByShareCode(shareCode: string): Promise<SavedLoadout | undefined> {
    const [loadout] = await db.select().from(savedLoadouts).where(eq(savedLoadouts.shareCode, shareCode));
    return loadout;
  }

  async createLoadout(loadout: InsertLoadout): Promise<SavedLoadout> {
    const [created] = await db.insert(savedLoadouts).values(loadout).returning();
    return created;
  }

  async deleteLoadout(id: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await db.delete(savedLoadouts).where(eq(savedLoadouts.id, id)).returning();
    return result.length > 0;
  }

  async createSession(session: InsertSession): Promise<Session> {
    const [created] = await db.insert(sessions).values(session).returning();
    return created;
  }

  async getSession(id: string): Promise<Session | undefined> {
    const [session] = await db.select().from(sessions).where(eq(sessions.id, id));
    return session;
  }

  async updateSessionTokens(id: string, accessToken: string, refreshToken: string | null, tokenExpiresAt: Date): Promise<void> {
    await db.update(sessions).set({ accessToken, refreshToken, tokenExpiresAt }).where(eq(sessions.id, id));
  }

  async deleteSession(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await db.delete(sessions).where(eq(sessions.id, id));
  }

  async deleteExpiredSessions(): Promise<void> {
    const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    await db.delete(sessions).where(lt(sessions.createdAt, cutoff));
  }

  async getAiUsage(bungieMembershipId: string, date: string): Promise<AiUsage | undefined> {
    const [usage] = await db.select().from(aiUsage).where(
      and(eq(aiUsage.bungieMembershipId, bungieMembershipId), eq(aiUsage.usageDate, date))
    );
    return usage;
  }

  async incrementAiUsage(bungieMembershipId: string, date: string): Promise<AiUsage> {
    const [result] = await db.insert(aiUsage)
      .values({ bungieMembershipId, usageDate: date, requestCount: 1 })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: [aiUsage.bungieMembershipId, aiUsage.usageDate],
        set: { requestCount: sql`${aiUsage.requestCount} + 1` },
      })
      .returning();
    return result;
  }

  async getPremiumStatus(bungieMembershipId: string): Promise<UserPremium | undefined> {
    const [premium] = await db.select().from(userPremium).where(eq(userPremium.bungieMembershipId, bungieMembershipId));
    return premium;
  }

  async setPremiumStatus(bungieMembershipId: string, isPremium: boolean, premiumUntil?: Date): Promise<UserPremium> {
    const existing = await this.getPremiumStatus(bungieMembershipId);
    if (existing) {
      const [updated] = await db.update(userPremium)
        .set({
          isPremium: isPremium ? 1 : 0,
          premiumSince: isPremium && !existing.premiumSince ? new Date() : existing.premiumSince,
          premiumUntil: premiumUntil || existing.premiumUntil,
        })
        .where(eq(userPremium.id, existing.id))
        .returning();
      return updated;
    }
    const [created] = await db.insert(userPremium)
      .values({
        bungieMembershipId,
        isPremium: isPremium ? 1 : 0,
        premiumSince: isPremium ? new Date() : undefined,
        premiumUntil,
      })
      .returning();
    return created;
  }
}

export const storage = new DatabaseStorage();

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: server/static.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import express, { type Express } from "express";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

export function serveStatic(app: Express) {
  const distPath = path.resolve(__dirname, "public");
  if (!fs.existsSync(distPath)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Could not find the build directory: ${distPath}, make sure to build the client first`,
    );
  }

  app.use(express.static(distPath));

  // fall through to index.html if the file doesn't exist
  app.use("/{*path}", (_req, res) => {
    res.sendFile(path.resolve(distPath, "index.html"));
  });
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: server/vite.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { type Express } from "express";
import { createServer as createViteServer, createLogger } from "vite";
import { type Server } from "http";
import viteConfig from "../vite.config";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

const viteLogger = createLogger();

export async function setupVite(server: Server, app: Express) {
  const serverOptions = {
    middlewareMode: true,
    hmr: { server, path: "/vite-hmr" },
    allowedHosts: true as const,
  };

  const vite = await createViteServer({
    ...viteConfig,
    configFile: false,
    customLogger: {
      ...viteLogger,
      error: (msg, options) => {
        viteLogger.error(msg, options);
        process.exit(1);
      },
    },
    server: serverOptions,
    appType: "custom",
  });

  app.use(vite.middlewares);

  app.use("/{*path}", async (req, res, next) => {
    const url = req.originalUrl;

    try {
      const clientTemplate = path.resolve(
        import.meta.dirname,
        "..",
        "client",
        "index.html",
      );

      // always reload the index.html file from disk incase it changes
      let template = await fs.promises.readFile(clientTemplate, "utf-8");
      template = template.replace(
        `src="/src/main.tsx"`,
        `src="/src/main.tsx?v=${nanoid()}"`,
      );
      const page = await vite.transformIndexHtml(url, template);
      res.status(200).set({ "Content-Type": "text/html" }).end(page);
    } catch (e) {
      vite.ssrFixStacktrace(e as Error);
      next(e);
    }
  });
}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { Switch, Route } from "wouter";
import { queryClient } from "./lib/queryClient";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { I18nProvider } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/ErrorBoundary";
import NotFound from "@/pages/not-found";
import Home from "@/pages/Home";

import Dashboard from "@/pages/Dashboard";
import SharePage from "@/pages/SharePage";
import DemoDashboard from "@/pages/Demo";
import { ImprintPage, PrivacyPage } from "@/pages/Legal";

function Router() {
  return (
    <Switch>
      <Route path="/" component={Home} />
      <Route path="/dashboard" component={Dashboard} />
      <Route path="/share/:shareCode" component={SharePage} />
      <Route path="/demo" component={DemoDashboard} />
      <Route path="/imprint" component={ImprintPage} />
      <Route path="/privacy" component={PrivacyPage} />
      <Route component={NotFound} />
    </Switch>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <I18nProvider>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <TooltipProvider>
          <ErrorBoundary fallbackTitle="GuardianGrid encountered an error">
            <Toaster />
            <Router />
            <div className="noise-overlay" aria-hidden="true" />
          </ErrorBoundary>
        </TooltipProvider>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </I18nProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

interface I18nContextType {
  language: Language;
  setLanguage: (lang: Language) => void;
  t: (key: string) => string;
}

const I18nContext = createContext<I18nContextType | null>(null);

export function I18nProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<Language>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('guardiangrid_language') || localStorage.getItem('forge_language');
    if (saved && !localStorage.getItem('guardiangrid_language')) {
      localStorage.setItem('guardiangrid_language', saved);
      localStorage.removeItem('forge_language');
    }
    return (saved as Language) || 'de';
  });

  const setLanguage = useCallback((lang: Language) => {
    setLanguageState(lang);
    localStorage.setItem('guardiangrid_language', lang);
  }, []);

  const t = useCallback((key: string): string => {
    return translations[language]?.[key] || translations.de[key] || key;
  }, [language]);

  return (
    <I18nContext.Provider value={{ language, setLanguage, t }}>
      {children}
    </I18nContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useI18n() {
  const ctx = useContext(I18nContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useI18n must be used within I18nProvider');
  return ctx;
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/lib/queryClient.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { QueryClient, QueryFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";

async function throwIfResNotOk(res: Response) {
  if (!res.ok) {
    let errorData: any;
    try {
      errorData = await res.json();
    } catch {
      errorData = { message: res.statusText };
    }
    const error = new Error(errorData.message || `${res.status}: ${res.statusText}`);
    (error as any).status = res.status;
    (error as any).errorType = errorData.errorType || null;
    (error as any).errorCode = errorData.errorCode || 0;
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(
  method: string,
  url: string,
  data?: unknown | undefined,
): Promise<Response> {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: data ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {},
    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    credentials: "include",
  });

  await throwIfResNotOk(res);
  return res;
}

type UnauthorizedBehavior = "returnNull" | "throw";
export const getQueryFn: <T>(options: {
  on401: UnauthorizedBehavior;
}) => QueryFunction<T> =
  ({ on401: unauthorizedBehavior }) =>
  async ({ queryKey }) => {
    const res = await fetch(queryKey.join("/") as string, {
      credentials: "include",
    });

    if (unauthorizedBehavior === "returnNull" && res.status === 401) {
      return null;
    }

    await throwIfResNotOk(res);
    return await res.json();
  };

function shouldRetry(failureCount: number, error: any): boolean {
  if (error?.status === 401 || error?.status === 403) return false;
  if (error?.status === 429) return false;
  if (error?.errorType === 'auth') return false;
  if (error?.errorType === 'maintenance') return false;
  if (error?.errorType === 'rate_limit') return false;
  return failureCount < 2;
}

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      queryFn: getQueryFn({ on401: "throw" }),
      refetchInterval: false,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
      retry: shouldRetry,
      retryDelay: (attemptIndex) => Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, attemptIndex), 8000),
      networkMode: 'offlineFirst',
    },
    mutations: {
      retry: false,
      networkMode: 'always',
    },
  },
});

    'Pugilist', 'Grave Robber',
  ]),
};

const UNIVERSAL_S_TIER = new Set([
  'Vorpal Weapon', 'Frenzy', 'Incandescent', 'Destabilizing Rounds',
  'Hatchling', 'Firing Line', 'Bait and Switch',
]);

const UNIVERSAL_A_TIER = new Set([
  'Demolitionist', 'Rapid Hit', 'Subsistence', 'Perpetual Motion',
  'Auto-Loading Holster', 'Reconstruction',
]);

const WEAPON_TYPE_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  'Hand Cannon': 'hand_cannon',
  'Pulse Rifle': 'pulse_rifle',
  'Auto Rifle': 'auto_rifle',
  'Scout Rifle': 'scout_rifle',
  'Submachine Gun': 'submachine_gun',
  'Sidearm': 'sidearm',
  'Shotgun': 'shotgun',
  'Sniper Rifle': 'sniper_rifle',
  'Fusion Rifle': 'fusion_rifle',
  'Trace Rifle': 'trace_rifle',
  'Linear Fusion Rifle': 'linear_fusion_rifle',
  'Grenade Launcher': 'grenade_launcher',
  'Rocket Launcher': 'rocket_launcher',
  'Sword': 'sword',
  'Bow': 'bow',
  'Combat Bow': 'bow',
  'Machine Gun': 'machine_gun',
  'Glaive': 'glaive',
};

export type RollRating = 'META' | 'GOOD' | 'MID';

export interface RollResult {
  rating: RollRating;
  sPerks: string[];
  aPerks: string[];
}

export function rateWeaponRoll(
  weaponType: string,
  perkNames: string[]
): RollResult {
  const typeKey = WEAPON_TYPE_MAP[weaponType];
  const sTierSet = typeKey ? S_TIER_PERKS[typeKey] : undefined;
  const aTierSet = typeKey ? A_TIER_PERKS[typeKey] : undefined;

  const sPerks: string[] = [];
  const aPerks: string[] = [];

  for (const perk of perkNames) {
    if (sTierSet?.has(perk) || UNIVERSAL_S_TIER.has(perk)) {
      sPerks.push(perk);
    } else if (aTierSet?.has(perk) || UNIVERSAL_A_TIER.has(perk)) {
      aPerks.push(perk);
    }
  }

  let rating: RollRating;
  if (sPerks.length >= 3 || (sPerks.length >= 2 && aPerks.length >= 1)) {
    rating = 'META';
  } else if (sPerks.length >= 1 || aPerks.length >= 2) {
    rating = 'GOOD';
  } else {
    rating = 'MID';
  }

  return { rating, sPerks, aPerks };
}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/hooks/use-bungie-auth.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';

const BUNGIE_CLIENT_ID = '51625';
const BUNGIE_AUTH_URL = `https://www.bungie.net/en/OAuth/Authorize?client_id=${BUNGIE_CLIENT_ID}&response_type=code`;

export interface DestinyProfile {
  membershipId: string;
  membershipType: number;
  displayName: string;
  emblemPath?: string;
}

export function useBungieAuth() {
  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const [isAuthenticating, setIsAuthenticating] = useState(false);
  const [destinyProfile, setDestinyProfile] = useState<DestinyProfile | null>(null);
  const [isFetchingProfile, setIsFetchingProfile] = useState(true);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const hasAttemptedAuth = useRef(false);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const authCode = urlParams.get('code');

    if (authCode && !hasAttemptedAuth.current) {
      hasAttemptedAuth.current = true;
      setIsAuthenticating(true);
      window.history.replaceState({}, document.title, window.location.pathname);
      exchangeCodeForToken(authCode);
    } else {
      checkExistingSession();
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, []);

  const checkExistingSession = useCallback(async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/me', {
        credentials: 'include',
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        const profile: DestinyProfile = {
          membershipId: data.membershipId,
          membershipType: data.membershipType,
          displayName: data.displayName,
          emblemPath: data.iconPath,
        };
        setDestinyProfile(profile);
        setIsAuthenticated(true);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === 'AbortError') return;
    } finally {
      setIsFetchingProfile(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const exchangeCodeForToken = useCallback(async (code: string) => {
    abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ code }),
        credentials: 'include',
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.message || 'Login failed');
      }

      const data = await res.json();
      const profile: DestinyProfile = {
        membershipId: data.membershipId,
        membershipType: data.membershipType,
        displayName: data.displayName,
        emblemPath: data.iconPath,
      };

      setDestinyProfile(profile);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.name === 'AbortError') return;
      console.error('Auth error:', err);
      setAuthError('Anmeldung fehlgeschlagen. Bitte versuche es erneut.');
    } finally {
      setIsAuthenticating(false);
      setIsFetchingProfile(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(() => {
    window.location.href = BUNGIE_AUTH_URL;
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    try {
      await fetch('/api/auth/logout', {
        method: 'POST',
        credentials: 'include',
      });
    } catch {}
    setDestinyProfile(null);
    setIsAuthenticated(false);
  }, []);

  return {
    isAuthenticated,
    isAuthenticating,
    isFetchingProfile,
    destinyProfile,
    authError,
    login,
    logout
  };
}

export type BungieErrorType = 'rate_limit' | 'maintenance' | 'auth' | 'timeout' | 'network' | 'api' | null;

export function getBungieErrorType(error: unknown): BungieErrorType {
  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') return null;
  return (error as any).errorType || null;
}

export function useProfileState(membershipType?: number, membershipId?: string) {
  return useQuery<ProfileState>({
    queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state', membershipType, membershipId],
    queryFn: async ({ signal }) => {
      if (!membershipType || !membershipId) {
        return { characters: [], vaultItems: [], postmasterItems: [], currencies: [], collectibles: [], records: [], artifact: undefined, inGameLoadouts: {} };
      }

      const res = await fetch(
        `/api/bungie/profile-state/${membershipType}/${membershipId}`,
        {
          credentials: 'include',
          signal,
        }
      );

      if (!res.ok) {
        let errorData: any;
        try {
          errorData = await res.json();
        } catch {
          errorData = { message: 'Unknown error' };
        }
        const error = new Error(errorData.message || 'Failed to fetch profile state');
        (error as any).status = res.status;
        (error as any).errorType = errorData.errorType || null;
        (error as any).errorCode = errorData.errorCode || 0;
        throw error;
      }

      return res.json();
    },
    enabled: !!membershipType && !!membershipId,
    staleTime: 2 * 60 * 1000,
    refetchInterval: 5 * 60 * 1000,
    refetchIntervalInBackground: false,
    retry: (failureCount, error: any) => {
      if (error?.errorType === 'auth' || error?.errorType === 'maintenance') return false;
      if (error?.status === 401) return false;
      return failureCount < 1;
    },
  });
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/hooks/use-loadouts.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api, buildUrl, type LoadoutInput } from "@shared/routes";

export function useLoadouts(bungieMembershipId?: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [api.loadouts.list.path, bungieMembershipId],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!bungieMembershipId) return [];
      const url = buildUrl(api.loadouts.list.path, { bungieMembershipId });
      const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: "include" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to fetch loadouts");
      return api.loadouts.list.responses[200].parse(await res.json());
    },
    enabled: !!bungieMembershipId,
  });
}

export function useSaveLoadout() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (data: LoadoutInput) => {
      const validated = api.loadouts.create.input.parse(data);
      const res = await fetch(api.loadouts.create.path, {
        method: api.loadouts.create.method,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(validated),
        credentials: "include",
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (res.status === 400) {
          const error = api.loadouts.create.responses[400].parse(await res.json());
          throw new Error(error.message);
        }
        throw new Error("Failed to save loadout");
      }
      
      return api.loadouts.create.responses[201].parse(await res.json());
    },
    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: [api.loadouts.list.path, variables.bungieMembershipId],
      });
    },
  });
}

export function useDeleteLoadout() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, bungieMembershipId }: { id: number; bungieMembershipId: string }) => {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/loadouts/${id}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        credentials: 'include',
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete loadout');
      return res.json();
    },
    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        queryKey: [api.loadouts.list.path, variables.bungieMembershipId],
      });
    },
  });
}

FILE: client/src/hooks/use-transfer.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient, useIsMutating } from '@tanstack/react-query';

const PROFILE_SYNC_CHANNEL = 'guardiangrid-profile-sync';

let bc: BroadcastChannel | null = null;
try {
  bc = new BroadcastChannel(PROFILE_SYNC_CHANNEL);
} catch {}

function notifyOtherTabs() {
  try {
    bc?.postMessage({ type: 'profile-invalidate' });
  } catch {}
}

export function useProfileTabSync() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!bc) return;
    const handler = (event: MessageEvent) => {
      if (event.data?.type === 'profile-invalidate') {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state'] });
      }
    };
    bc.addEventListener('message', handler);
    return () => bc?.removeEventListener('message', handler);
  }, [queryClient]);
}

interface TransferParams {
  itemReferenceHash: number;
  itemId: string;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
  transferToVault: boolean;
}

  itemId: string;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

  item: {
    id: string;
    itemHash: number;
    location: string;
    characterId?: string;
  };
  targetCharacterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

  itemId: string;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

  itemId: string;
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
  state: boolean;

  itemIds: string[];
  characterId: string;
  membershipType: number;
}

const BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY = ['bungie-mutation'];

async function bungiePostRequest(url: string, body: any, signal?: AbortSignal) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    credentials: 'include',
    signal,
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    let errorData: any;
    try {
      errorData = await res.json();
    } catch {
      errorData = { message: 'Request failed' };
    }
    const error = new Error(errorData.message || 'Request failed');
    (error as any).status = res.status;
    (error as any).errorType = errorData.errorType || null;
    throw error;
  }
  return res.json();
}

export function useIsBungieMutating() {
  return useIsMutating({ mutationKey: BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY }) > 0;
}

export function useTransferItem() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY,
    mutationFn: async (params: TransferParams) => {
      return bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/transfer', params);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state'] });
      notifyOtherTabs();
    },
  });
}

export function useEquipItem() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY,
    mutationFn: async (params: EquipParams) => {
      return bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/equip', params);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state'] });
      notifyOtherTabs();
    },
  });
}

export function useSwapItem() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY,
    mutationFn: async (params: SwapParams) => {
      const { item, targetCharacterId, membershipType } = params;

      if (item.location === 'vault') {
        await bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/transfer', {
          itemReferenceHash: item.itemHash,
          itemId: item.id,
          characterId: targetCharacterId,
          membershipType,
          transferToVault: false,
        });

        await bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/equip', {
          itemId: item.id,
          characterId: targetCharacterId,
          membershipType,
        });

      } else if (item.location === 'character' && item.characterId === targetCharacterId) {
        await bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/equip', {
          itemId: item.id,
          characterId: targetCharacterId,
          membershipType,
        });

      } else if (item.location === 'character' && item.characterId !== targetCharacterId) {
        await bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/transfer', {
          itemReferenceHash: item.itemHash,
          itemId: item.id,
          characterId: item.characterId!,
          membershipType,
          transferToVault: true,
        });

        await bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/transfer', {
          itemReferenceHash: item.itemHash,
          itemId: item.id,
          characterId: targetCharacterId,
          membershipType,
          transferToVault: false,
        });

        await bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/equip', {
          itemId: item.id,
          characterId: targetCharacterId,
          membershipType,
        });
      }

      return { success: true };
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state'] });
      notifyOtherTabs();
    },
  });
}

export function usePullFromPostmaster() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY,
    mutationFn: async (params: PullPostmasterParams) => {
      return bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/pull-postmaster', params);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state'] });
      notifyOtherTabs();
    },
  });
}

export function useEquipLoadout() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: BUNGIE_MUTATION_KEY,
    mutationFn: async (params: EquipLoadoutParams) => {
      return bungiePostRequest('/api/bungie/equip-loadout', params);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['/api/bungie/profile-state'] });
      notifyOtherTabs();
    },
  });
}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/hooks/use-online-status.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export function useOnlineStatus() {
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(
    typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOnline = () => setIsOnline(true);
    const handleOffline = () => setIsOnline(false);

    window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.addEventListener('offline', handleOffline);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
      window.removeEventListener('offline', handleOffline);
    };
  }, []);

  return isOnline;
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/hooks/use-scroll-reveal.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useRef, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';

export function useScrollReveal(threshold = 0.15) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;

    const scrollRoot = el.closest('[data-scroll-container]') as HTMLElement | null;

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          setIsVisible(true);
          observer.unobserve(el);
        }
      },
      { threshold, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px', root: scrollRoot }
    );

    observer.observe(el);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [threshold]);

  return { ref, isVisible };
}

export function useParallax(speed = 0.3) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;

    const parent = el.closest('[data-scroll-container]') || el.parentElement;
    if (!parent) return;

    let rafId: number;
    const handleScroll = () => {
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const windowH = window.innerHeight;
        const center = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
        const delta = (center - windowH / 2) / windowH;
        setOffset(delta * speed * 100);
      });
    };

    parent.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });
    handleScroll();
    return () => {
      parent.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    };
  }, [speed]);

  return { ref, offset };
}

export function useStaggerChildren() {
  const getDelay = useCallback((index: number, baseDelay = 0.04) => {
    return index * baseDelay;
  }, []);

  return { getDelay };
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/hooks/use-toast.ts
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import * as React from "react"

import type {
  ToastActionElement,
  ToastProps,
} from "@/components/ui/toast"

const TOAST_LIMIT = 1
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000

type ToasterToast = ToastProps & {
  id: string
  title?: React.ReactNode
  description?: React.ReactNode
  action?: ToastActionElement
}

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: "ADD_TOAST",
  UPDATE_TOAST: "UPDATE_TOAST",
  DISMISS_TOAST: "DISMISS_TOAST",
  REMOVE_TOAST: "REMOVE_TOAST",
} as const

let count = 0

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  return count.toString()
}

type ActionType = typeof actionTypes

type Action =
  | {
      type: ActionType["ADD_TOAST"]
      toast: ToasterToast
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType["UPDATE_TOAST"]
      toast: Partial<ToasterToast>
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType["DISMISS_TOAST"]
      toastId?: ToasterToast["id"]
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType["REMOVE_TOAST"]
      toastId?: ToasterToast["id"]
    }

interface State {
  toasts: ToasterToast[]
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>()

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId: string) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId)
    dispatch({
      type: "REMOVE_TOAST",
      toastId: toastId,
    })
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY)

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout)
}

export const reducer = (state: State, action: Action): State => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "ADD_TOAST":
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      }

    case "UPDATE_TOAST":
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t
        ),
      }

    case "DISMISS_TOAST": {
      const { toastId } = action

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId)
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id)
        })
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
                ...t,
                open: false,
              }
            : t
        ),
      }
    }
    case "REMOVE_TOAST":
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        }
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      }
  }
}

const listeners: Array<(state: State) => void> = []

let memoryState: State = { toasts: [] }

function dispatch(action: Action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action)
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState)
  })
}

type Toast = Omit<ToasterToast, "id">

function toast({ ...props }: Toast) {
  const id = genId()

  const update = (props: ToasterToast) =>
    dispatch({
      type: "UPDATE_TOAST",
      toast: { ...props, id },
    })
  const dismiss = () => dispatch({ type: "DISMISS_TOAST", toastId: id })

  dispatch({
    type: "ADD_TOAST",
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss()
      },
    },
  })

  return {
    id: id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  }
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<State>(memoryState)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState)
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState)
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1)
      }
    }
  }, [state])

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId?: string) => dispatch({ type: "DISMISS_TOAST", toastId }),
  }
}

export { useToast, toast }


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/hooks/use-mobile.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange)
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { Component, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, Home } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallbackTitle?: string;
  onReset?: () => void;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  constructor(props: Props) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: { componentStack?: string | null }) {
    console.error('[ErrorBoundary]', error, info.componentStack);
  }

  handleRetry = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
    this.props.onReset?.();
  };

  handleGoHome = () => {
    window.location.href = '/';
  };

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[300px] p-8"
          data-testid="error-boundary-fallback"
        >
          <div className="relative mb-6">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-8 h-8 text-red-400" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white/90 mb-2">
            {this.props.fallbackTitle || 'Something went wrong'}
          </h2>

          <p className="text-sm text-white/50 mb-6 max-w-md text-center">
            {this.state.error?.message || 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.'}
          </p>

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button
              onClick={this.handleRetry}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/10 border border-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/20 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] text-sm hover:bg-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/20 transition-colors"
              data-testid="button-error-retry"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
              Retry
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={this.handleGoHome}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-white/70 text-sm hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
              data-testid="button-error-home"
            >
              <Home className="w-4 h-4" />
              Go Home
            </button>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

import { ChevronRight, LogOut } from 'lucide-react';

function bungieIcon(url: string | undefined): string {
  if (!url) return '';
  if (url.startsWith('http')) return url;
  return `https://www.bungie.net${url}`;
}

interface NavButtonProps {
  icon: React.ElementType;
  label: string;
  active: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  collapsed?: boolean;
}

export function DesktopNavButton({ icon: Icon, label, active, onClick, collapsed }: NavButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button 
      onClick={onClick}
      title={collapsed ? label : undefined}
      className={`w-full flex items-center ${collapsed ? 'justify-center px-2' : 'space-x-3 px-4'} py-3 transition-all duration-300 relative overflow-hidden group sidebar-shine-hover ${
        active 
          ? 'text-white' 
          : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-white border-l-2 border-transparent'
      }`}
    >
      {active && (
        <motion.div
          layoutId="sidebar-active-bg"
          className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500/15 via-purple-500/10 to-transparent border-l-2 border-indigo-400"
          initial={false}
          transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
        />
      )}
      {active && (
        <motion.div
          layoutId="sidebar-glow"
          className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-24 h-12 bg-indigo-500/20 blur-xl rounded-full"
          initial={false}
          transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
        />
      )}
      {!active && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-white/0 via-white/0 to-transparent group-hover:from-white/[0.04] group-hover:via-white/[0.02] transition-all duration-500 border-l-2 border-transparent group-hover:border-white/20" />
      )}
      <div className={`relative z-10 flex items-center ${collapsed ? 'justify-center' : 'space-x-3'} w-full`}>
        <div className={`relative ${active ? 'text-indigo-400' : 'group-hover:text-indigo-300'} transition-colors duration-300`}>
          <Icon className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={1.5} />
          {active && (
            <motion.div
              className="absolute -inset-1.5 bg-indigo-400/15 rounded-md blur-sm"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.5 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
            />
          )}
        </div>
        {!collapsed && <span className="text-sm font-display tracking-destiny font-medium">{label}</span>}
        {active && !collapsed && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -4 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 0.5, x: 0 }}
            className="ml-auto"
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import React from 'react';
import { Crosshair, Sparkles, Package, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from 'lucide-react';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';

export interface ItemStatValue {
