  center: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
  exit: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? -60 : 60, opacity: 0 }),
};

function RatingRing({ rating, size = 96 }: { rating: number; size?: number }) {
  const strokeWidth = 6;
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const progress = (rating / 10) * circumference;
  const color = rating >= 8 ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : rating >= 6 ? '#818cf8' : rating >= 4 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';

  return (
    <div className="relative" style={{ width: size, height: size }} data-testid="rating-ring">
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
        <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke="white" strokeOpacity={0.06} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
        <motion.circle
          cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} strokeLinecap="round"
          initial={{ strokeDasharray: circumference, strokeDashoffset: circumference }}
          animate={{ strokeDashoffset: circumference - progress }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <motion.span
          className="text-2xl font-bold text-white"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.5 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.5, type: 'spring', damping: 20, stiffness: 200 }}
        >
          {rating}
        </motion.span>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide">/10</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PlaystylePanel({ tabDirection, selectedCharacter, artifact }: PlaystylePanelProps) {
  const { t, language } = useI18n();
  const [analysis, setAnalysis] = useState<PlaystyleAnalysis | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const runAnalysis = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedCharacter) return;
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const characterData = {
      className: selectedCharacter.className,
      raceName: selectedCharacter.raceName,
      light: selectedCharacter.light,
      stats: selectedCharacter.stats.map(s => ({ name: s.name, value: s.value })),
      equippedItems: selectedCharacter.equippedItems.map(item => ({
        slot: item.slot,
        name: item.name,
        type: item.type,
        rarity: item.rarity,
        power: item.power,
        perks: (item.perks || []).map(p => ({ name: p.name, isActive: p.isActive })),
        mods: (item.mods || []).map(m => ({ name: m.name })),
      })),
      subclass: (() => {
        const subclassItem = selectedCharacter.equippedItems.find(i => i.slot === 'subclass');
        if (!subclassItem) return null;
        return {
          name: subclassItem.name,
          sockets: (subclassItem.sockets || []).map(s => ({ name: s.name, category: s.description || 'ability' })),
        };
      })(),
      artifact: artifact ? {
        powerBonus: artifact.powerBonus,
        activeMods: artifact.tiers.flatMap(tier => tier.items.filter(i => i.isActive).map(i => i.name)),
      } : null,
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiRequest('POST', '/api/ai/analyze-playstyle', { characterData, language });
      const data = await res.json();
      setAnalysis(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message?.includes('429') || err.message?.includes('rate limit')) {
        setError(t('analysis.rateLimit'));
      } else {
        setError(t('analysis.error'));
      }
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [selectedCharacter, artifact, language, t]);

  return (
    <motion.div
      key="playstyle"
      custom={tabDirection}
      variants={tabVariants}
      initial="enter"
      animate="center"
      exit="exit"
      transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
      className="h-full overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden scroll-container px-4 md:px-8 py-6"
      data-scroll-container
      data-testid="panel-playstyle"
    >
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"
        >
          <Brain className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400" />
          <h2 className="font-display text-sm tracking-destiny-wide text-white uppercase">{t('analysis.title')}</h2>
        </motion.div>

        {!analysis && !isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/10 flex items-center justify-center animate-breathe">
              <Brain className="w-10 h-10 text-indigo-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm max-w-md">{t('analysis.description')}</p>
              {selectedCharacter && (
                <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs">{selectedCharacter.className} · {selectedCharacter.light} Power</p>
              )}
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={runAnalysis}
              disabled={!selectedCharacter}
              className="press-scale px-8 py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase transition-all duration-300 destiny-clip-sm border border-indigo-500/30"
              data-testid="button-start-analysis"
            >
              {t('analysis.start')}
            </button>
            {error && (
              <motion.p
                initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                className="text-red-400 text-xs flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {error}
              </motion.p>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-400 animate-spin" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -inset-2 rounded-full border border-indigo-500/20 animate-ping" style={{ animationDuration: '2s' }} />
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase">{t('analysis.loading')}</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {analysis && !isLoading && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-6 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
                <RatingRing rating={analysis.rating} />
                <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.3, duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                  >
                    <span className="inline-block px-3 py-1 bg-indigo-500/20 border border-indigo-500/30 rounded-full text-indigo-300 text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase" data-testid="text-playstyle-label">
                      {analysis.playstyle}
                    </span>
                  </motion.div>
                  <motion.p
                    initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.5, duration: 0.5 }}
                    className="text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed"
                    data-testid="text-playstyle-summary"
                  >
                    {analysis.summary}
                  </motion.p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.2 }}
                className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                  <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-emerald-400 uppercase">{t('analysis.strengths')}</h3>
                </div>
                <ul className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-strengths">
                  {analysis.strengths.map((s, i) => (
                    <motion.li
                      key={i}
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                      transition={{ delay: 0.3 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                      className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                    >
                      <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                      {s}
                    </motion.li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </motion.div>

              <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
                className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-amber-400 uppercase">{t('analysis.weaknesses')}</h3>
                </div>
                <ul className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-weaknesses">
                  {analysis.weaknesses.map((w, i) => (
                    <motion.li
                      key={i}
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                      transition={{ delay: 0.4 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                      className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                    >
                      <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                      {w}
                    </motion.li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </motion.div>
            </div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.4 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-purple-400 uppercase">{t('analysis.synergies')}</h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-synergies">
                {analysis.synergies.map((s, i) => (
                  <motion.li
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.5 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                  >
                    <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-500/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    {s}
                  </motion.li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.5 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <Lightbulb className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] uppercase">{t('analysis.tips')}</h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-tips">
                {analysis.tips.map((tip, i) => (
                  <motion.li
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.6 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                  >
                    <Target className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    {tip}
                  </motion.li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.7, duration: 0.5 }}
              className="flex items-center justify-center pt-2 pb-8"
            >
              <button
                onClick={runAnalysis}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-reanalyze"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('analysis.rerun')}
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/components/dashboard/BuildAdvisorPanel.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { appleEase } from './shared';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Swords, Shield, Target, Crosshair, Skull, Castle, Loader2, AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, ChevronRight, Flame, Gem, TrendingUp, Lightbulb } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import type { CharacterData, InventoryItem, ArtifactInfo } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { apiRequest } from '@/lib/queryClient';

type ActivityType = 'raid' | 'nightfall' | 'pvp' | 'gambit' | 'dungeon';

interface BuildRecommendation {
  activity: string;
  recommendedWeapons: { slot: string; name: string; reason: string }[];
  recommendedArmor: { slot: string; name: string; reason: string }[];
  recommendedSubclass: { name: string; aspects: string[]; fragments: string[] };
  statPriorities: string[];
  strategy: string;
}

interface BuildAdvisorPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  selectedCharacter: CharacterData | null;
  vaultItems: InventoryItem[];
  artifact?: ArtifactInfo;
}


const tabVariants = {
  enter: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? 60 : -60, opacity: 0 }),
  center: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
  exit: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? -60 : 60, opacity: 0 }),
};

const activities: { id: ActivityType; icon: typeof Swords; color: string }[] = [
  { id: 'raid', icon: Castle, color: 'var(--color-gold-accent)' },
  { id: 'nightfall', icon: Skull, color: '#818cf8' },
  { id: 'pvp', icon: Crosshair, color: '#ef4444' },
  { id: 'gambit', icon: Gem, color: '#22c55e' },
  { id: 'dungeon', icon: Flame, color: '#f59e0b' },
];


export function BuildAdvisorPanel({ tabDirection, selectedCharacter, vaultItems, artifact }: BuildAdvisorPanelProps) {
  const { t, language } = useI18n();
  const [selectedActivity, setSelectedActivity] = useState<ActivityType>('raid');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const cachedResults = useRef<Record<string, BuildRecommendation>>({});
  const [result, setResult] = useState<BuildRecommendation | null>(null);

  const generateRecommendation = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedCharacter) return;

    const cacheKey = `${selectedActivity}-${selectedCharacter.characterId}-${language}`;
    if (cachedResults.current[cacheKey]) {
      setResult(cachedResults.current[cacheKey]);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const allItems = [...selectedCharacter.equippedItems, ...selectedCharacter.inventory, ...vaultItems];

    const availableWeapons = allItems
      .filter(i => ['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'].includes(i.slot))
      .map(w => ({
        name: w.name,
        type: w.type,
        slot: w.slot,
        rarity: w.rarity,
        power: w.power,
        perks: (w.perks || []).filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name),
      }));

    const availableArmor = allItems
      .filter(i => ['helmet', 'arms', 'chest', 'legs', 'class'].includes(i.slot))
      .map(a => ({
        name: a.name,
        slot: a.slot,
        rarity: a.rarity,
      }));

    const subclassItem = selectedCharacter.equippedItems.find(i => i.slot === 'subclass');
    const subclass = subclassItem ? {
      name: subclassItem.name,
      sockets: (subclassItem.sockets || []).map(s => ({ name: s.name, category: s.description || 'ability' })),
    } : null;

    try {
      const res = await apiRequest('POST', '/api/ai/recommend-build', {
        buildData: {
          activity: selectedActivity,
          className: selectedCharacter.className,
          light: selectedCharacter.light,
          stats: selectedCharacter.stats.map(s => ({ name: s.name, value: s.value })),
          availableWeapons,
          availableArmor,
          subclass,
        },
        language,
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      cachedResults.current[cacheKey] = data;
      setResult(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message?.includes('429') || err.message?.includes('rate limit')) {
        setError(t('analysis.rateLimit'));
      } else {
        setError(t('analysis.error'));
      }
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [selectedCharacter, selectedActivity, vaultItems, language, t]);

  const slotIcons: Record<string, typeof Swords> = {
    kinetic: Target,
    energy: Flame,
    power: Skull,
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      key="build-advisor"
      custom={tabDirection}
      variants={tabVariants}
      initial="enter"
      animate="center"
      exit="exit"
      transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
      className="h-full overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden scroll-container px-4 md:px-8 py-6"
      data-scroll-container
      data-testid="panel-build-advisor"
    >
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"
        >
          <Swords className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400" />
          <h2 className="font-display text-sm tracking-destiny-wide text-white uppercase" data-testid="text-build-advisor-title">
            {t('build.title')}
          </h2>
        </motion.div>

        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap"
          data-testid="activity-selector"
        >
          {activities.map((act) => {
            const Icon = act.icon;
            const isSelected = selectedActivity === act.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={act.id}
                onClick={() => { setSelectedActivity(act.id); setResult(null); setError(null); }}
                className={`press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase transition-all duration-300 border ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-white/20 bg-white/[0.08] text-white'
                    : 'border-white/[0.06] bg-white/[0.02] text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300 hover:bg-white/[0.04]'
                }`}
                style={isSelected ? { borderColor: `${act.color}40`, boxShadow: `0 0 12px ${act.color}15` } : {}}
                data-testid={`button-activity-${act.id}`}
              >
                <Icon className="w-4 h-4" style={isSelected ? { color: act.color } : {}} />
                <span>{t(`build.activity.${act.id}`)}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </motion.div>

        {!result && !isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.15 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/10 flex items-center justify-center animate-breathe">
              <Swords className="w-10 h-10 text-indigo-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm max-w-md" data-testid="text-build-description">
                {t('build.description')}
              </p>
              {selectedCharacter && (
                <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs">{selectedCharacter.className} · {selectedCharacter.light} Power</p>
              )}
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={generateRecommendation}
              disabled={!selectedCharacter}
              className="press-scale px-8 py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase transition-all duration-300 destiny-clip-sm border border-indigo-500/30"
              data-testid="button-generate-build"
            >
              {t('build.generate')}
            </button>
            {error && (
              <motion.p
                initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                className="text-red-400 text-xs flex items-center gap-2"
                data-testid="text-build-error"
              >
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {error}
              </motion.p>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-400 animate-spin" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -inset-2 rounded-full border border-indigo-500/20 animate-ping" style={{ animationDuration: '2s' }} />
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase" data-testid="text-build-loading">
              {t('build.loading')}
            </p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {result && !isLoading && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                <Swords className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-400" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-indigo-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-weapons-heading">
                  {t('build.weapons')}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-3" data-testid="list-recommended-weapons">
                {result.recommendedWeapons.map((w, i) => {
                  const SlotIcon = slotIcons[w.slot] || Target;
                  return (
                    <motion.li
                      key={i}
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                      transition={{ delay: 0.2 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                      className="flex items-start gap-3"
                      data-testid={`item-weapon-${i}`}
                    >
                      <SlotIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-400/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-sm text-white font-medium">{w.name}</span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-gray-600 ml-2 uppercase tracking-wider">{w.slot}</span>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{w.reason}</p>
                      </div>
                    </motion.li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.2 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-emerald-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-armor-heading">
                  {t('build.armor')}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-3" data-testid="list-recommended-armor">
                {result.recommendedArmor.map((a, i) => (
                  <motion.li
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.3 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-3"
                    data-testid={`item-armor-${i}`}
                  >
                    <Shield className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-sm text-white font-medium">{a.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-600 ml-2 uppercase tracking-wider">{a.slot}</span>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{a.reason}</p>
                    </div>
                  </motion.li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <Flame className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-purple-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-subclass-heading">
                  {t('build.subclass')}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <p className="text-sm text-white font-medium" data-testid="text-subclass-name">{result.recommendedSubclass.name}</p>
                {result.recommendedSubclass.aspects.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                      {t('build.aspects')}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-1">
                      {result.recommendedSubclass.aspects.map((asp, i) => (
                        <span key={i} className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-purple-500/15 border border-purple-500/20 rounded-full text-purple-300 text-xs" data-testid={`badge-aspect-${i}`}>
                          {asp}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                {result.recommendedSubclass.fragments.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                      {t('build.fragments')}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-1">
                      {result.recommendedSubclass.fragments.map((frag, i) => (
                        <span key={i} className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/15 rounded-full text-purple-300/80 text-xs" data-testid={`badge-fragment-${i}`}>
                          {frag}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.4 }}
                className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                  <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" />
                  <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] uppercase" data-testid="text-stats-heading">
                    {t('build.stats')}
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <ol className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-stat-priorities">
                  {result.statPriorities.map((stat, i) => (
                    <motion.li
                      key={i}
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                      transition={{ delay: 0.5 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                      data-testid={`item-stat-${i}`}
                    >
                      <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] text-xs font-bold shrink-0">
                        {i + 1}
                      </span>
                      {stat}
                    </motion.li>
                  ))}
                </ol>
              </motion.div>

              <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.5 }}
                className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                  <Lightbulb className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-amber-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-strategy-heading">
                    {t('build.strategy')}
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-300 leading-relaxed" data-testid="text-strategy-content">
                  {result.strategy}
                </p>
              </motion.div>
            </div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.6, duration: 0.5 }}
              className="flex items-center justify-center pt-2 pb-8"
            >
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  const cacheKey = `${selectedActivity}-${selectedCharacter?.characterId}-${language}`;
                  delete cachedResults.current[cacheKey];
                  setResult(null);
                  setError(null);
                }}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-regenerate-build"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('build.regenerate')}
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/components/dashboard/WeaponRollPanel.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { appleEase } from './shared';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Crosshair, Search, Loader2, AlertTriangle, ChevronRight, Star, ChevronDown, RefreshCw, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import type { InventoryItem } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { apiRequest } from '@/lib/queryClient';

function bungieIcon(url: string | undefined): string {
  if (!url) return '';
  if (url.startsWith('http')) return url;
  return `https://www.bungie.net${url}`;
}

interface PerkBreakdown {
  name: string;
  rating: 's' | 'a' | 'b' | 'c' | 'f';
  explanation: string;
}

interface WeaponRollEvaluation {
  verdict: 'god_roll' | 'great' | 'good' | 'average' | 'shard';
  score: number;
  perkBreakdown: PerkBreakdown[];
  idealPerks: string[];
  summary: string;
}

interface WeaponRollPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  vaultItems: InventoryItem[];
  characterItems: InventoryItem[];
}


const tabVariants = {
  enter: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? 60 : -60, opacity: 0 }),
  center: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
  exit: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? -60 : 60, opacity: 0 }),
};

const WEAPON_SLOTS = ['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'];

const verdictConfig: Record<string, { label: string; color: string; bgColor: string; borderColor: string }> = {
  god_roll: { label: 'GOD ROLL', color: 'var(--color-gold-accent)', bgColor: 'rgba(229, 199, 107, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(229, 199, 107, 0.3)' },
  great: { label: 'GREAT', color: '#818cf8', bgColor: 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.3)' },
  good: { label: 'GOOD', color: '#34d399', bgColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.3)' },
  average: { label: 'AVERAGE', color: '#f59e0b', bgColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3)' },
  shard: { label: 'SHARD', color: '#ef4444', bgColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)', borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)' },
};

const ratingColors: Record<string, string> = {
  s: 'var(--color-gold-accent)',
  a: '#818cf8',
  b: '#34d399',
  c: '#f59e0b',
  f: '#ef4444',
};

function RatingRing({ rating, size = 96 }: { rating: number; size?: number }) {
  const strokeWidth = 6;
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const progress = (rating / 10) * circumference;
  const color = rating >= 8 ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : rating >= 6 ? '#818cf8' : rating >= 4 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';

  return (
    <div className="relative" style={{ width: size, height: size }} data-testid="rating-ring-weapon">
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
        <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke="white" strokeOpacity={0.06} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
        <motion.circle
          cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} strokeLinecap="round"
          initial={{ strokeDasharray: circumference, strokeDashoffset: circumference }}
          animate={{ strokeDashoffset: circumference - progress }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <motion.span
          className="text-2xl font-bold text-white"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.5 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.5, type: 'spring', damping: 20, stiffness: 200 }}
        >
          {rating}
        </motion.span>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide">/10</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function WeaponRollPanel({ tabDirection, vaultItems, characterItems }: WeaponRollPanelProps) {
  const { t, language } = useI18n();
  const [selectedWeapon, setSelectedWeapon] = useState<InventoryItem | null>(null);
  const [evaluation, setEvaluation] = useState<WeaponRollEvaluation | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [cache, setCache] = useState<Record<string, WeaponRollEvaluation>>({});

  const allWeapons = useMemo(() => {
    const weapons = [...characterItems, ...vaultItems].filter(
      item => WEAPON_SLOTS.includes(item.slot) && (item.rarity === 'legendary' || item.rarity === 'exotic')
    );
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    return weapons.filter(w => {
      if (seen.has(w.id)) return false;
      seen.add(w.id);
      return true;
    });
  }, [vaultItems, characterItems]);

  const filteredWeapons = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchQuery) return allWeapons;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return allWeapons.filter(w =>
      w.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || w.type.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [allWeapons, searchQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const evaluateRoll = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedWeapon) return;

    if (cache[selectedWeapon.id]) {
      setEvaluation(cache[selectedWeapon.id]);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const weaponData = {
      name: selectedWeapon.name,
      type: selectedWeapon.type,
      slot: selectedWeapon.slot,
      rarity: selectedWeapon.rarity,
      power: selectedWeapon.power,
      perks: (selectedWeapon.perks || []).map((p, idx) => ({
        name: p.name,
        isActive: p.isActive,
        column: idx,
      })),
      mods: (selectedWeapon.mods || []).map(m => ({ name: m.name })),
      masterwork: '',
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiRequest('POST', '/api/ai/evaluate-roll', { weaponData, language });
      const data = await res.json();
      setEvaluation(data);
      setCache(prev => ({ ...prev, [selectedWeapon.id]: data }));
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message?.includes('429') || err.message?.includes('rate limit')) {
        setError(t('analysis.rateLimit'));
      } else {
        setError(t('analysis.error'));
      }
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [selectedWeapon, language, t, cache]);

  const selectWeapon = useCallback((weapon: InventoryItem) => {
    setSelectedWeapon(weapon);
    setIsDropdownOpen(false);
    setSearchQuery('');
    setEvaluation(null);
    setError(null);

    if (cache[weapon.id]) {
      setEvaluation(cache[weapon.id]);
    }
  }, [cache]);

  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedWeapon(null);
    setEvaluation(null);
    setError(null);
  }, []);

  return (
    <motion.div
      key="weapon-roll"
      custom={tabDirection}
      variants={tabVariants}
      initial="enter"
      animate="center"
      exit="exit"
      transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
      className="h-full overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden scroll-container px-4 md:px-8 py-6"
      data-scroll-container
      data-testid="panel-weapon-roll"
    >
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"
        >
          <Crosshair className="w-5 h-5 text-orange-400" />
          <h2 className="font-display text-sm tracking-destiny-wide text-white uppercase" data-testid="text-weapon-roll-title">{t('roll.title')}</h2>
        </motion.div>

        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
          className="space-y-4"
        >
          <div ref={dropdownRef} className="relative">
            <div
              className={`flex items-center gap-3 bg-white/[0.03] border rounded-lg px-4 py-3 cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-200 ${isDropdownOpen ? 'border-orange-500/40' : 'border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/[0.12]'}`}
              onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(!isDropdownOpen)}
              data-testid="dropdown-weapon-selector"
            >
              {selectedWeapon ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1 min-w-0">
                  {selectedWeapon.icon && (
                    <img
                      src={bungieIcon(selectedWeapon.icon)}
                      alt=""
                      className="w-8 h-8 rounded"
                    />
                  )}
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm text-white truncate" data-testid="text-selected-weapon">{selectedWeapon.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{selectedWeapon.type} · {selectedWeapon.power}</p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); clearSelection(); }}
                    className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                    data-testid="button-clear-weapon"
                  >
                    <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1">
                  <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-500" />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">{t('roll.selectWeapon')}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-gray-500 transition-transform duration-200 ${isDropdownOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
            </div>

            <AnimatePresence>
              {isDropdownOpen && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                  className="absolute z-50 w-full mt-2 bg-[#0d1117] border border-white/[0.08] rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-h-72 flex flex-col"
                >
                  <div className="p-2 border-b border-white/[0.06]">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white/[0.04] rounded-md px-3 py-2">
                      <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={searchQuery}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder={t('roll.searchWeapons')}
                        className="bg-transparent text-sm text-white placeholder-gray-500 outline-none flex-1"
                        autoFocus
                        data-testid="input-weapon-search"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="overflow-y-auto scroll-container flex-1">
                    {filteredWeapons.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="p-4 text-center text-xs text-gray-500">
                        {t('item.noneAvailable')}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      filteredWeapons.map((weapon) => (
                        <button
                          key={weapon.id}
                          onClick={() => selectWeapon(weapon)}
                          className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-white/[0.04] ${selectedWeapon?.id === weapon.id ? 'bg-white/[0.06]' : ''}`}
                          data-testid={`option-weapon-${weapon.id}`}
                        >
                          {weapon.icon && (
                            <img
                              src={bungieIcon(weapon.icon)}
                              alt=""
                              className={`w-8 h-8 rounded ${weapon.rarity === 'exotic' ? 'ring-1 ring-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/30' : ''}`}
                            />
                          )}
                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-200 truncate">{weapon.name}</p>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{weapon.type} · {weapon.power}</p>
                          </div>
                          <span className={`text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider ${weapon.rarity === 'exotic' ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/60' : 'text-purple-400/60'}`}>
                            {weapon.rarity}
                          </span>
                          {cache[weapon.id] && (
                            <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400/60" />
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ))
                    )}
                  </div>
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>

          {selectedWeapon && !evaluation && !isLoading && (
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
              className="flex flex-col items-center py-10 space-y-4"
            >
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Crosshair className="w-8 h-8 text-orange-400" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm text-center max-w-sm">{t('roll.description')}</p>
              <button
                onClick={evaluateRoll}
                className="press-scale px-8 py-3 bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase transition-all duration-300 destiny-clip-sm border border-orange-500/30"
                data-testid="button-evaluate-roll"
              >
                {t('roll.evaluate')}
              </button>
              {error && (
                <motion.p
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  className="text-red-400 text-xs flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {error}
                </motion.p>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {!selectedWeapon && !isLoading && (
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
              className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
            >
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center animate-breathe">
                <Crosshair className="w-10 h-10 text-orange-400" />
              </div>
              <div className="text-center space-y-2">
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm max-w-md">{t('roll.description')}</p>
                <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs">{allWeapons.length} {t('roll.weaponsAvailable')}</p>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </motion.div>

        {isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-orange-400 animate-spin" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -inset-2 rounded-full border border-orange-500/20 animate-ping" style={{ animationDuration: '2s' }} />
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase">{t('roll.loading')}</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {evaluation && !isLoading && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
              className={`bg-white/[0.03] border rounded-xl p-6 hover-lift ${evaluation.verdict === 'god_roll' ? 'godroll-glow' : 'border-white/[0.06]'}`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
                <RatingRing rating={evaluation.score} />
                <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.3, duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap"
                  >
                    <span
                      className="inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase"
                      style={{
                        color: verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.color,
                        backgroundColor: verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.bgColor,
                        borderWidth: 1,
                        borderColor: verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.borderColor,
                      }}
                      data-testid="badge-verdict"
                    >
                      {verdictConfig[evaluation.verdict]?.label || evaluation.verdict}
                    </span>
                    {selectedWeapon && (
                      <span className="text-gray-500 text-xs">{selectedWeapon.name}</span>
                    )}
                  </motion.div>
                  <motion.p
                    initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.5, duration: 0.5 }}
                    className="text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed"
                    data-testid="text-roll-summary"
                  >
                    {evaluation.summary}
                  </motion.p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.2 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                <Crosshair className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-400" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-orange-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-perk-breakdown-title">{t('roll.perkBreakdown')}</h3>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-3" data-testid="list-perk-breakdown">
                {evaluation.perkBreakdown.map((perk, i) => (
                  <motion.div
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.3 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-white/[0.02] rounded-lg border border-white/[0.04]"
                    data-testid={`perk-breakdown-${i}`}
                  >
                    <span
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-7 h-7 rounded-md text-xs font-bold uppercase shrink-0"
                      style={{
                        color: ratingColors[perk.rating] || '#888',
                        backgroundColor: `${ratingColors[perk.rating] || '#888'}15`,
                        borderWidth: 1,
                        borderColor: `${ratingColors[perk.rating] || '#888'}30`,
                      }}
                      data-testid={`badge-perk-rating-${i}`}
                    >
                      {perk.rating.toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className="text-sm text-white font-medium" data-testid={`text-perk-name-${i}`}>{perk.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">{perk.explanation}</p>
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
              className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" />
                <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] uppercase" data-testid="text-ideal-perks-title">{t('roll.idealPerks')}</h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-2" data-testid="list-ideal-perks">
                {evaluation.idealPerks.map((perk, i) => (
                  <motion.li
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.4 + i * 0.08, duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-300"
                  >
                    <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    {perk}
                  </motion.li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </motion.div>

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.5, duration: 0.5 }}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2 pb-8 flex-wrap"
            >
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  if (selectedWeapon) {
                    setCache(prev => {
                      const next = { ...prev };
                      delete next[selectedWeapon.id];
                      return next;
                    });
                    setEvaluation(null);
                  }
                }}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-reevaluate"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('roll.reevaluate')}
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={clearSelection}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-select-another"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('roll.selectAnother')}
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/components/dashboard/TriumphAdvisorPanel.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { appleEase } from './shared';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Trophy, AlertTriangle, Loader2, Clock, Lightbulb, ChevronRight, Star, RefreshCw, Target, ChevronDown, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import type { CharacterData, RecordItem } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { apiRequest } from '@/lib/queryClient';

interface TriumphSuggestion {
  name: string;
  reason: string;
  estimatedTime: string;
  tips: string[];
}

interface TriumphAdvisorResult {
  prioritizedTriumphs: TriumphSuggestion[];
  generalAdvice: string;
}

interface TriumphAdvisorPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  selectedCharacter: CharacterData | null;
  records: RecordItem[];
}


const tabVariants = {
  enter: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? 60 : -60, opacity: 0 }),
  center: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
  exit: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? -60 : 60, opacity: 0 }),
};

function findMatchingRecord(name: string, records: RecordItem[]): RecordItem | null {
  const lower = name.toLowerCase().trim();
  const exact = records.find(r => r.name.toLowerCase().trim() === lower);
  if (exact) return exact;
  const partial = records.find(r =>
    r.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || lower.includes(r.name.toLowerCase())
  );
  return partial || null;
}

function RecordDetail({ record }: { record: RecordItem }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.35, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
      className="overflow-hidden"
    >
      <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-white/[0.06]">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-3">
          <div className={`w-12 h-12 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden ring-1 rounded ${
            record.completed ? 'ring-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/30' : 'ring-white/10'
          }`}>
            {record.icon ? (
              <img src={record.icon} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" loading="lazy" />
            ) : (
              <Trophy className={`w-6 h-6 ${record.completed ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]' : 'text-gray-600'}`} />
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h4 className={`text-xs font-bold tracking-wider uppercase truncate ${
                record.completed ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]' : 'text-white'
              }`}>{record.name}</h4>
              {record.completed && (
                <div className="shrink-0 w-4 h-4 bg-green-500/80 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-white" />
                </div>
              )}
              {record.scoreValue > 0 && (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/60 shrink-0">{record.scoreValue} pts</span>
              )}
            </div>
            {record.description && (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{record.description}</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {record.objectives.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-2.5 mt-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Target className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-400" />
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny font-display">{t('triumph.objectives')}</span>
            </div>
            {record.objectives.map((obj, i) => {
              const pct = obj.completionValue > 0 ? Math.min(100, (obj.progress / obj.completionValue) * 100) : 0;
              return (
                <div key={i} className="bg-white/[0.02] border border-white/[0.04] rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300 flex-1 truncate">{obj.description || t('triumphs.progress')}</span>
                    <span className={`text-[11px] shrink-0 ml-2 font-mono ${obj.complete ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>
                      {obj.progress.toLocaleString()}/{obj.completionValue.toLocaleString()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="h-1.5 bg-white/[0.06] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <motion.div
                      initial={{ width: 0 }}
                      animate={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                      transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
                      className={`h-full rounded-full ${obj.complete ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'}`}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export function TriumphAdvisorPanel({ tabDirection, selectedCharacter, records }: TriumphAdvisorPanelProps) {
  const { t, language } = useI18n();
  const [result, setResult] = useState<TriumphAdvisorResult | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [expandedIndex, setExpandedIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const incompleteRecords = records.filter(r => !r.completed);

  const matchedRecords = useMemo(() => {
    if (!result) return new Map<number, RecordItem | null>();
    const map = new Map<number, RecordItem | null>();
    result.prioritizedTriumphs.forEach((triumph, i) => {
      map.set(i, findMatchingRecord(triumph.name, records));
    });
    return map;
  }, [result, records]);

  const runAnalysis = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedCharacter || incompleteRecords.length === 0) return;
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setExpandedIndex(null);

    const triumphData = {
      incompleteRecords: incompleteRecords.slice(0, 30).map(r => ({
        name: r.name,
        description: r.description,
        objectives: r.objectives.map(o => ({
          description: o.description,
          progress: o.progress,
          completionValue: o.completionValue,
          complete: o.complete,
        })),
      })),
      className: selectedCharacter.className,
      equippedItems: selectedCharacter.equippedItems.map(item => ({
        name: item.name,
        type: item.type,
        slot: item.slot,
      })),
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiRequest('POST', '/api/ai/triumph-advisor', { triumphData, language });
      const data = await res.json();
      setResult(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message?.includes('429') || err.message?.includes('rate limit')) {
        setError(t('analysis.rateLimit'));
      } else {
        setError(t('analysis.error'));
      }
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [selectedCharacter, incompleteRecords, language, t]);

  const toggleExpand = (index: number) => {
    setExpandedIndex(prev => prev === index ? null : index);
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      key="triumph-advisor"
      custom={tabDirection}
      variants={tabVariants}
      initial="enter"
      animate="center"
      exit="exit"
      transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
      className="h-full overflow-y-auto scroll-container px-4 md:px-8 py-6"
      data-scroll-container
      data-testid="panel-triumph-advisor"
    >
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[] }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"
        >
          <Trophy className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
          <h2 className="font-display text-sm tracking-destiny-wide text-white uppercase" data-testid="text-triumph-advisor-title">{t('triumph.title')}</h2>
        </motion.div>

        {!result && !isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 flex items-center justify-center animate-breathe">
              <Trophy className="w-10 h-10 text-amber-400" />
            </div>
            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm max-w-md" data-testid="text-triumph-advisor-desc">{t('triumph.description')}</p>
              {selectedCharacter && (
                <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs">
                  {selectedCharacter.className} · {incompleteRecords.length} {t('triumph.incompleteCount')}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={runAnalysis}
              disabled={!selectedCharacter || incompleteRecords.length === 0}
              className="press-scale px-8 py-3 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase transition-all duration-300 destiny-clip-sm border border-amber-500/30"
              data-testid="button-analyze-triumphs"
            >
              {t('triumph.analyze')}
            </button>
            {error && (
              <motion.p
                initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                className="text-red-400 text-xs flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {error}
              </motion.p>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {isLoading && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 space-y-6"
          >
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -inset-2 rounded-full border border-amber-500/20 animate-ping" style={{ animationDuration: '2s' }} />
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-display tracking-destiny-wide uppercase" data-testid="text-triumph-loading">{t('triumph.loading')}</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {result && !isLoading && (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {result.prioritizedTriumphs.map((triumph, i) => {
                const matchedRecord = matchedRecords.get(i) ?? null;
                const isExpanded = expandedIndex === i;

                return (
                  <motion.div
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.1 + i * 0.08 }}
                    className={`bg-white/[0.03] border rounded-xl p-5 transition-all duration-300 ${
                      isExpanded ? 'border-amber-500/30 bg-white/[0.04]' : 'border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/[0.12]'
                    } ${matchedRecord ? 'cursor-pointer' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => matchedRecord && toggleExpand(i)}
                    data-testid={`card-triumph-${i}`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                      <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-amber-500/15 flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5">
                        <span className="text-amber-400 text-xs font-bold">#{i + 1}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <h3 className="text-white text-sm font-medium" data-testid={`text-triumph-name-${i}`}>{triumph.name}</h3>
                            {matchedRecord && (
                              <motion.div
                                animate={{ rotate: isExpanded ? 180 : 0 }}
                                transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
                              >
                                <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
                              </motion.div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-gray-500 shrink-0">
                            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span className="text-xs" data-testid={`text-triumph-time-${i}`}>{triumph.estimatedTime}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                          <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500/60 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                          <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed" data-testid={`text-triumph-reason-${i}`}>{triumph.reason}</p>
                        </div>

                        {triumph.tips.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <Lightbulb className="w-3 h-3 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/70" />
                              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny font-display">{t('triumph.tips')}</span>
                            </div>
                            <ul className="space-y-1">
                              {triumph.tips.map((tip, j) => (
                                <li key={j} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-gray-400">
                                  <ChevronRight className="w-3 h-3 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/40 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                                  <span data-testid={`text-triumph-tip-${i}-${j}`}>{tip}</span>
                                </li>
                              ))}
                            </ul>
                          </div>
                        )}

                        <AnimatePresence>
                          {isExpanded && matchedRecord && (
                            <RecordDetail record={matchedRecord} />
                          )}
                        </AnimatePresence>

                        {matchedRecord && !isExpanded && (
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 italic mt-1">{t('triumph.clickToExpand')}</p>
                        )}
                        {!matchedRecord && (
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 italic mt-1">{t('triumph.noMatch')}</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {result.generalAdvice && (
              <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.5 }}
                className="bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover-lift"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                  <Target className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-display text-xs tracking-destiny text-indigo-400 uppercase" data-testid="text-triumph-advice-title">{t('triumph.advice')}</h3>
                </div>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed" data-testid="text-triumph-advice">{result.generalAdvice}</p>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            <motion.div
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.7, duration: 0.5 }}
              className="flex items-center justify-center pt-2 pb-8"
            >
              <button
                onClick={runAnalysis}
                className="press-scale flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.08] text-gray-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-display tracking-destiny uppercase transition-all duration-300 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-lg"
                data-testid="button-reanalyze-triumphs"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('triumph.reanalyze')}
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/components/dashboard/SynergyPanel.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { appleEase } from './shared';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { Link2, Zap, AlertTriangle, ArrowRightLeft, RefreshCw, Loader2, Shield, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import type { CharacterData } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { apiRequest } from '@/lib/queryClient';

interface SynergyCombo {
  name: string;
  strength: 'strong' | 'moderate' | 'weak';
  items: string[];
  perks: string[];
  explanation: string;
}

interface AntiSynergy {
  issue: string;
  items: string[];
  explanation: string;
  fix: string;
}

interface SwapSuggestion {
  current: string;
  recommended: string;
  reason: string;
}

interface SynergyAnalysis {
  overallRating: number;
  synergies: SynergyCombo[];
  antiSynergies: AntiSynergy[];
  suggestions: SwapSuggestion[];
  summary: string;
}

interface SynergyPanelProps {
  tabDirection: number;
  selectedCharacter: CharacterData | null;
}


const tabVariants = {
  enter: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? 60 : -60, opacity: 0 }),
  center: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
  exit: (dir: number) => ({ x: dir > 0 ? -60 : 60, opacity: 0 }),
};

function RatingRing({ rating, size = 96 }: { rating: number; size?: number }) {
  const strokeWidth = 6;
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const progress = (rating / 10) * circumference;
  const color = rating >= 8 ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : rating >= 6 ? '#818cf8' : rating >= 4 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';

  return (
    <div className="relative" style={{ width: size, height: size }} data-testid="synergy-rating-ring">
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
        <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke="white" strokeOpacity={0.06} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
        <motion.circle
          cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} strokeLinecap="round"
          initial={{ strokeDasharray: circumference, strokeDashoffset: circumference }}
          animate={{ strokeDashoffset: circumference - progress }}
          transition={{ duration: 1.5, ease: appleEase as unknown as number[], delay: 0.3 }}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <motion.span
          className="text-2xl font-bold text-white"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.5 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.5, type: 'spring', damping: 20, stiffness: 200 }}
        >
          {rating}
        </motion.span>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide">/10</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StrengthBadge({ strength, t }: { strength: 'strong' | 'moderate' | 'weak'; t: (key: string) => string }) {
  const config = {
    strong: { bg: 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30', text: 'text-emerald-400' },
    moderate: { bg: 'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/30', text: 'text-amber-400' },
    weak: { bg: 'bg-gray-500/10 border-gray-500/30', text: 'text-gray-400' },
  };
  const c = config[strength];
  return (
    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-display uppercase tracking-destiny border rounded-full ${c.bg} ${c.text}`} data-testid={`badge-strength-${strength}`}>
      {t(`synergy.strength.${strength}`)}
    </span>
  );
}

export default function SynergyPanel({ tabDirection, selectedCharacter }: SynergyPanelProps) {
  const { t, language } = useI18n();
  const [result, setResult] = useState<SynergyAnalysis | null>(null);
