                        selectedCharacterId === char.characterId
                          ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600/20 to-transparent border-indigo-500/40 text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-900/20'
                          : 'bg-white/[0.03] border-white/[0.06] text-gray-400 hover:border-white/20 hover:bg-white/[0.05]'
                      }`}
                      data-testid={`button-demo-char-${char.characterId}`}>
                      {selectedCharacterId === char.characterId && <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-[2px] bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-500" />}
                      {char.emblemPath && <img src={char.emblemPath} alt="" className="w-9 h-9 ring-1 ring-white/10" />}
                      <div className="text-left">
                        <p className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase">{char.className}</p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" /> {char.light}</p>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                {selectedCharacter && (
                  <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
                    <motion.div key={selectedCharacter.characterId} initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} transition={{ duration: 0.4 }} className="relative overflow-hidden">
                      {selectedCharacter.emblemBackgroundPath && <img src={selectedCharacter.emblemBackgroundPath} alt="" className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover opacity-60" />}
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-black/80 via-black/40 to-transparent" />
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#080b14] via-transparent to-transparent" />
                      <div className="relative z-10 p-5 md:p-7 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <motion.div initial={{ x: -20, opacity: 0 }} animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }} transition={{ delay: 0.1, duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }}>
                          <h3 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl text-white font-display font-light tracking-wider text-shadow-glow">{selectedCharacter.className}</h3>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-destiny-widest mt-1">{selectedCharacter.raceName}</p>
                        </motion.div>
                        <motion.div className="flex items-center gap-4" initial={{ x: 20, opacity: 0 }} animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }} transition={{ delay: 0.15, duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }}>
                          {(() => {
                            const subclassItem = selectedCharacter.equippedItems.find(i => i.slot === 'subclass');
                            return subclassItem?.icon ? <img src={subclassItem.icon} alt={subclassItem.name} title={subclassItem.name} className="w-12 h-12 drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(100,180,255,0.4)]" /> : null;
                          })()}
                          <div className="text-right">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 justify-end">
                              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] animate-[pulse_3s_ease-in-out_infinite]" />
                              <span className="text-4xl font-display font-light text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] text-shadow-glow">{selectedCharacter.light}</span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide mt-1.5">{t('power.label')}</p>
                          </div>
                        </motion.div>
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>

                    {selectedCharacter.stats.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="px-4 md:px-6 pb-5">
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-6 gap-1.5">
                          {selectedCharacter.stats.map((stat, statIdx) => {
                            const val = stat.value;
                            const pct = Math.min((val / 200) * 100, 100);
                            const isMax = val >= 200;
                            const barColor = isMax ? 'from-[var(--color-gold-accent)] via-[var(--color-gold-accent)] to-[var(--color-gold-accent)]'
                              : val >= 100 ? 'from-purple-500 to-violet-400'
                              : val >= 60 ? 'from-emerald-600 to-green-400'
                              : val >= 20 ? 'from-yellow-600 to-amber-400'
                              : 'from-red-700 to-red-400';
                            const numColor = isMax ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]'
                              : val >= 100 ? 'text-purple-300'
                              : val >= 60 ? 'text-emerald-300'
                              : val >= 20 ? 'text-amber-300'
                              : 'text-red-400';
                            return (
                              <motion.div key={stat.name} initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 12 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.2 + statIdx * 0.06, duration: 0.4, ease: 'easeOut' }}
                                className={`bg-black/30 border p-2 hover:border-white/15 transition-all duration-300 ${isMax ? 'border-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/25' : 'border-white/[0.06]'}`} title={stat.description}>
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1.5">
                                  {stat.icon && <img src={stat.icon} alt={stat.name} className="w-4 h-4 opacity-70" />}
                                  <p className={`text-[11px] uppercase tracking-destiny truncate flex-1 ${isMax ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/60' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>{stat.name}</p>
                                  <p className={`text-sm font-display font-light leading-none ${numColor}`}>{val}</p>
                                </div>
                                <div className="w-full h-1 bg-white/[0.06] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                                  <div className={`h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r ${barColor} transition-all duration-500`} style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
                                </div>
                              </motion.div>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.3, duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }} className="px-4 md:px-6 pb-5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                        <Crosshair className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-400/70" />
                        <h4 className="text-[11px] text-white/70 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide font-display">{t('section.weapons')}</h4>
                        <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-white/[0.06]" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        {(['kinetic', 'energy', 'power'] as const).map(slotId => {
                          const equipped = selectedCharacter.equippedItems.find(i => i.slot === slotId);
                          if (!equipped) return null;
                          const isExotic = equipped.rarity === 'exotic';
                          const rarityColor = isExotic ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : 'var(--color-legendary)';
                          const perkNames = [
                            ...(equipped.perks || []).filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name),
                            ...(equipped.mods || []).map(m => m.name),
                          ];
                          const rollResult = perkNames.length > 0 ? rateWeaponRoll(equipped.type, perkNames) : null;
                          return (
                            <div key={slotId} className={`group flex items-center gap-3 p-3 border transition-all duration-300 relative overflow-hidden bg-black/40 border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/20 hover:bg-white/[0.03]`}>
                              {equipped.screenshot && (
                                <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-[0.04] pointer-events-none">
                                  <img src={equipped.screenshot} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover blur-sm" />
                                </div>
                              )}
                              <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-[2px]" style={{ backgroundColor: rarityColor }} />
                              <div className={`w-12 h-12 shrink-0 overflow-hidden relative flex items-center justify-center ring-1 ring-white/10 ${isExotic ? 'rarity-exotic-glow' : 'rarity-legendary-glow'}`}>
                                <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-[2px] z-10" style={{ backgroundColor: rarityColor }} />
                                <img src={equipped.icon} alt={equipped.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 relative z-10">
                                <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide mb-0.5 font-medium">
                                  {slotId === 'kinetic' ? 'Primär' : slotId === 'energy' ? 'Energie' : 'Schwer'}
                                </p>
                                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold truncate tracking-wider uppercase" style={{ color: isExotic ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : 'white' }}>{equipped.name}</p>
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                                  <span className="text-[11px] text-yellow-500/80 flex items-center gap-0.5 font-medium">
                                    <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> {equipped.power}
                                  </span>
                                  <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-600">{equipped.type}</span>
                                  {rollResult && <DemoRollBadge result={rollResult} />}
                                </div>
                                {equipped.perks && equipped.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).length > 0 && (
                                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 flex-wrap">
                                    {equipped.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map((perk, i) => (
                                      <span key={i} className="text-[11px] px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-gray-400 rounded-sm uppercase tracking-wider">{perk.name}</span>
                                    ))}
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </div>
                              <button onClick={() => addToLoadout(toItem(equipped))} className="p-1.5 text-gray-600 hover:text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] hover:bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-sm opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all duration-200" title={t('action.toLoadout')}>
                                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>

                    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.45, duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }} className="px-4 md:px-6 pb-5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                        <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-400/70" />
                        <h4 className="text-[11px] text-white/70 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide font-display">{t('section.armor')}</h4>
                        <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-white/[0.06]" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        {(['helmet', 'arms', 'chest', 'legs', 'class'] as const).map(slotId => {
                          const equipped = selectedCharacter.equippedItems.find(i => i.slot === slotId);
                          if (!equipped) return null;
                          const isExotic = equipped.rarity === 'exotic';
                          const rarityColor = isExotic ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : 'var(--color-legendary)';
                          return (
                            <div key={slotId} className="group flex items-center gap-3 p-3 border transition-all duration-300 relative overflow-hidden bg-black/40 border-white/[0.06] hover:border-white/20 hover:bg-white/[0.03]">
                              <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-[2px]" style={{ backgroundColor: rarityColor }} />
                              <div className={`w-12 h-12 shrink-0 overflow-hidden relative flex items-center justify-center ring-1 ring-white/10 ${isExotic ? 'rarity-exotic-glow' : ''}`}>
                                <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-[2px] z-10" style={{ backgroundColor: rarityColor }} />
                                <img src={equipped.icon} alt={equipped.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 relative z-10">
                                <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide mb-0.5 font-medium">{equipped.type}</p>
                                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold truncate tracking-wider uppercase" style={{ color: isExotic ? 'var(--color-gold-accent)' : 'white' }}>{equipped.name}</p>
                                <span className="text-[11px] text-yellow-500/80 flex items-center gap-0.5 font-medium mt-0.5">
                                  <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> {equipped.power}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                              <button onClick={() => addToLoadout(toItem(equipped))} className="p-1.5 text-gray-600 hover:text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] hover:bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-sm opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all duration-200" title={t('action.toLoadout')}>
                                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>

                    {selectedCharacter.quests.length > 0 && (
                      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.55, duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeOut' }} className="px-4 md:px-6 pb-6">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                          <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-green-400/70" />
                          <h4 className="text-[11px] text-white/70 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide font-display">Aktive Quests</h4>
                          <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-white/[0.06]" />
                        </div>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                          {selectedCharacter.quests.map(quest => (
                            <div key={quest.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-white/[0.03] to-transparent border border-white/[0.06] p-3 hover:border-green-500/20 hover:from-green-500/[0.03] transition-all duration-300 group/quest">
                              <div className="w-10 h-10 shrink-0 ring-1 ring-white/10 overflow-hidden bg-black group-hover/quest:ring-green-500/20 transition-all">
                                {quest.icon && <img src={quest.icon} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />}
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold truncate tracking-wider uppercase text-white group-hover/quest:text-green-300/90 transition-colors">{quest.name}</p>
                                {quest.description && <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 truncate mt-0.5">{quest.description}</p>}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </motion.div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'vault' && (
              <motion.div key="vault" initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 30 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -30 }} transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] }} className="h-full flex flex-col lg:flex-row">
                <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 border-r border-white/5 min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="p-4 md:p-6 pb-2 shrink-0 space-y-3">
                    <div className="relative group">
                      <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-500 group-focus-within:text-indigo-400 transition-colors" />
                      <input type="text" placeholder={t('vault.search')} value={vaultFilters.search} onChange={(e) => setVaultFilters(f => ({ ...f, search: e.target.value }))}
                        className="w-full bg-[#0a0e18]/80 border border-white/[0.08] text-white pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500/40 transition-all font-light tracking-wider destiny-clip-sm" data-testid="demo-input-vault-search" />
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 tracking-widest uppercase">{filteredVaultItems.length} Gegenstände</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 pt-2">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-7 gap-2 md:gap-3">
                      {filteredVaultItems.map(item => (
                        <VaultItemCard
                          key={item.id}
                          item={item}
                          characters={demoCharacters}
                          onTransferToChar={() => toast({ title: 'Demo', description: 'Transfer nicht verfügbar im Demo-Modus' })}
                          onEquipOnChar={() => toast({ title: 'Demo', description: 'Equip nicht verfügbar im Demo-Modus' })}
                          onAddToLoadout={() => addToLoadout(toItem(item))}
                          isInLoadout={loadout[item.slot]?.id === item.id}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="hidden lg:flex w-[380px] bg-[#0a0e18]/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex-col shrink-0 relative overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="p-6 border-b border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h3 className="text-white font-display font-light tracking-destiny-wide flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
                      <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" /> AKTIVES LOADOUT
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-3">
                    {ITEM_SLOTS.map(slot => (
                      <EquippedSlot key={slot.id} slotInfo={slot} item={loadout[slot.id]} onUnequip={() => unequipItem(slot.id)} />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'collections' && (
              <motion.div key="collections" initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 30 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -30 }} transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] }} className="h-full flex flex-col">
                <div className="p-4 md:p-6 pb-2 shrink-0 space-y-3">
                  <div className="relative group">
                    <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-500" />
                    <input type="text" placeholder={t('collections.search')} value={collectionsSearch} onChange={(e) => setCollectionsSearch(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#0a0e18]/80 border border-white/[0.08] text-white pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500/40 transition-all font-light tracking-wider destiny-clip-sm" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {(['all', 'acquired', 'notAcquired'] as const).map(f => (
                      <button key={f} onClick={() => setCollectionsFilter(f)}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest border transition-all ${collectionsFilter === f ? 'bg-indigo-600/30 border-indigo-500/50 text-white' : 'bg-white/5 border-white/5 text-gray-500 hover:text-white'}`}>
                        {f === 'all' ? t('collections.all') : f === 'acquired' ? t('collections.acquired') : t('collections.notAcquired')}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 pt-2">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-2">
                    {filteredCollectibles.map(c => (
                      <div key={c.collectibleHash} className={`group relative border transition-all duration-300 destiny-clip-sm ${c.acquired ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-white/[0.04] to-transparent border-white/[0.08] hover:border-indigo-500/30' : 'bg-black/40 border-white/[0.04] opacity-40 grayscale hover:opacity-70'}`}>
                        <div className={`h-[2px] w-full ${c.acquired ? 'bg-indigo-500/60' : 'bg-gray-700/40'}`} />
                        <div className="aspect-square relative overflow-hidden bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-2">
                          {c.icon ? <img src={c.icon} alt={c.name} className="w-full h-full object-contain" /> : <BookOpen className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-700" />}
                          {c.acquired && <div className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-green-500/80 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"><Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-white" /></div>}
                        </div>
                        <div className="px-2 py-1.5">
                          <p className="text-[11px] font-bold truncate tracking-wider uppercase text-white">{c.name}</p>
                          {c.sourceString && <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 truncate mt-0.5">{c.sourceString}</p>}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'triumphs' && (
              <motion.div key="triumphs" initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 30 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -30 }} transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] }} className="h-full flex flex-col">
                <div className="p-4 md:p-6 pb-2 shrink-0 space-y-3">
                  <div className="relative group">
                    <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-500" />
                    <input type="text" placeholder={t('triumphs.search')} value={triumphsSearch} onChange={(e) => setTriumphsSearch(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#0a0e18]/80 border border-white/[0.08] text-white pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500/40 transition-all font-light tracking-wider destiny-clip-sm" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-6 pt-2">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {filteredRecords.map(record => (
                      <div key={record.recordHash} className={`border transition-all duration-300 p-3 ${record.completed ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/[0.06] to-transparent border-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/20' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-white/[0.02] to-transparent border-white/[0.06]'}`}>
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                          <div className={`w-10 h-10 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden ring-1 ${record.completed ? 'ring-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/30' : 'ring-white/10'}`}>
                            {record.icon ? <img src={record.icon} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <Trophy className={`w-5 h-5 ${record.completed ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]' : 'text-gray-600'}`} />}
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                              <h4 className={`text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase truncate ${record.completed ? 'text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]' : 'text-white'}`}>{record.name}</h4>
                              {record.completed && <div className="shrink-0 w-4 h-4 bg-green-500/80 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"><Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-white" /></div>}
                              {record.scoreValue > 0 && <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/60 shrink-0">{record.scoreValue} pts</span>}
                            </div>
                            {record.description && <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{record.description}</p>}
                            {record.objectives.length > 0 && !record.completed && (
                              <div className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
                                {record.objectives.map((obj, i) => {
                                  const pct = obj.completionValue > 0 ? Math.min(100, (obj.progress / obj.completionValue) * 100) : 0;
                                  return (
                                    <div key={i}>
                                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5">
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{obj.description || t('triumphs.progress')}</span>
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">{obj.progress}/{obj.completionValue}</span>
                                      </div>
                                      <div className="h-1 bg-white/[0.06] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                                        <div className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ${obj.complete ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-indigo-500'}`} style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
                                      </div>
                                    </div>
                                  );
                                })}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'season' && (
              <motion.div key="season" initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 30 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -30 }} transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] }} className="h-full overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-8">
                <div className="space-y-6">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                      <TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-400/70" />
                      <h4 className="text-[11px] text-white/70 uppercase tracking-destiny-wide font-display">{t('season.progressions')}</h4>
                      <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-white/[0.06]" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                      {DEMO_PROGRESSIONS.map(prog => {
                        const pct = prog.nextLevelAt > 0 ? Math.min(100, (prog.progressToNextLevel / prog.nextLevelAt) * 100) : 100;
                        return (
                          <div key={prog.progressionHash} className="bg-gradient-to-r from-white/[0.03] to-transparent border border-white/[0.06] p-3 hover:border-indigo-500/20 transition-all duration-300">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                              {prog.icon ? <img src={prog.icon} alt="" className="w-8 h-8 ring-1 ring-white/10" /> : <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-indigo-500/10 flex items-center justify-center ring-1 ring-white/10"><TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-400/50" /></div>}
                              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold truncate tracking-wider uppercase text-white">{prog.name}</p>
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                                  <span className="text-[11px] text-indigo-400">{t('season.level')} {prog.level}</span>
                                  {prog.currentResetCount > 0 && <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]">{t('season.resets')}: {prog.currentResetCount}</span>}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className="h-1.5 bg-white/[0.06] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                              <div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 mt-1 text-right">{prog.progressToNextLevel.toLocaleString()}/{prog.nextLevelAt > 0 ? prog.nextLevelAt.toLocaleString() : '—'}</p>
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}

            {(activeTab === 'loadouts' || activeTab === 'postmaster') && (
              <motion.div key={activeTab} initial={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * 30 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0, x: tabDirection * -30 }} transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] }} className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-500 space-y-6 p-8">
                {activeTab === 'loadouts' ? <Archive className="w-16 h-16 opacity-10" /> : <Mail className="w-16 h-16 opacity-10" />}
                <p className="font-display tracking-destiny-widest uppercase text-xs">Demo — {tabTitles[activeTab]}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 max-w-md text-center">
                  {activeTab === 'loadouts' ? 'Loadouts werden im echten Modus gespeichert und geladen.' : 'Postmeister-Items werden im echten Modus angezeigt.'}
                </p>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      </main>

      {(activeTab === 'vault' || activeTab === 'characters') && (
        <button onClick={() => setShowMobileLoadout(true)}
          className="lg:hidden fixed bottom-6 right-4 z-30 w-14 h-14 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-indigo-900/50 transition-all">
          <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5" />
          {Object.keys(loadout).length > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-5 h-5 bg-[var(--color-gold-accent)] text-black text-[11px] font-bold rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">{Object.keys(loadout).length}</span>
          )}
        </button>
      )}

      <AnimatePresence>
        {showMobileLoadout && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }} className="lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowMobileLoadout(false)}>
            <motion.div initial={{ y: '100%' }} animate={{ y: 0 }} exit={{ y: '100%' }} transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 300 }}
              className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 max-h-[85vh] bg-[#0a0e18] border-t border-white/[0.08] rounded-t-2xl flex flex-col"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center pt-3 pb-1"><div className="w-10 h-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full" /></div>
              <div className="p-4 border-b border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-white font-display font-light tracking-destiny-wide flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" /> AKTIVES LOADOUT
                </h3>
                <button onClick={() => setShowMobileLoadout(false)} className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white"><X className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-3">
                {ITEM_SLOTS.map(slot => (
                  <EquippedSlot key={slot.id} slotInfo={slot} item={loadout[slot.id]} onUnequip={() => unequipItem(slot.id)} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

function DemoRollBadge({ result }: { result: RollResult }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { rating, sPerks, aPerks } = result;

  const badgeStyle = rating === 'META'
    ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/20 to-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/20 border-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/40 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)] shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(229,199,107,0.25)]'
    : rating === 'GOOD'
    ? 'bg-purple-500/15 border-purple-500/30 text-purple-300'
    : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-gray-500';

  const label = rating === 'META' ? t('roll.meta') : rating === 'GOOD' ? t('roll.good') : t('roll.mid');

  return (
    <div className="group/roll relative">
      <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border rounded-sm ${badgeStyle}`}>
        {rating === 'META' && <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />}
        {label}
      </span>
      {(sPerks.length > 0 || aPerks.length > 0) && (
        <div className="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 mb-1.5 hidden group-hover/roll:block z-50 pointer-events-none">
          <div className="bg-black/95 border border-white/10 rounded px-2.5 py-2 min-w-[140px] max-w-[220px]">
            {sPerks.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mb-1">
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]/70 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">{t('roll.sPerks')}</p>
                {sPerks.map(p => <p key={p} className="text-[11px] text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]">{p}</p>)}
              </div>
            )}
            {aPerks.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-purple-400/70 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">{t('roll.aPerks')}</p>
                {aPerks.map(p => <p key={p} className="text-[11px] text-purple-300">{p}</p>)}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
FILE: client/src/pages/SharePage.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useRoute, useLocation } from 'wouter';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, buildUrl } from '@shared/routes';
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft, Hexagon, Sparkles, Check, AlertTriangle, X, ChevronDown, ArrowRight, Shield, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { type Item } from '@/components/Inventory';
import { SavedLoadout } from '@shared/schema';
import { useBungieAuth } from '@/hooks/use-bungie-auth';
import { useProfileState, type InventoryItem, type CharacterData } from '@/hooks/use-profile-state';
import { useTransferItem, useEquipItem } from '@/hooks/use-transfer';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { appleEase } from '@/components/dashboard/shared';

const ARMOR_SLOTS = ['helmet', 'arms', 'chest', 'legs', 'class'];
const STAT_NAMES = ['Mobility', 'Resilience', 'Recovery', 'Discipline', 'Intellect', 'Strength'];

type MatchStatus = 'exact' | 'substitute' | 'missing';
type ItemSource = 'equipped' | 'inventory' | 'vault' | 'other_character';

interface StatDiff {
  name: string;
  wanted: number;
  matched: number;
  diff: number;
}

interface MatchResult {
  slot: string;
  wanted: Item | null;
  matched: InventoryItem | null;
  status: MatchStatus;
  source?: ItemSource;
  sourceCharId?: string;
  note?: string;
  powerDiff?: number;
  statDiffs?: StatDiff[];
  perkMatchCount?: number;
  perkTotal?: number;
  matchScore?: number;
  substituteReason?: string;
}

function bungieIcon(url?: string) {
  if (!url) return '';
  if (url.startsWith('http')) return url;
  return `https://www.bungie.net${url}`;
}

function scoreCandidateMatch(wanted: Item, candidate: InventoryItem, isArmor: boolean): { score: number; perkMatches: number; perkTotal: number; modMatches: number; modTotal: number; statDiffs: StatDiff[] } {
  let score = 0;

  if (candidate.itemHash === wanted.itemHash) score += 15000;
  else if (candidate.name === wanted.name) score += 10000;
  if (candidate.rarity === wanted.rarity) score += 50;

  score += Math.max(0, candidate.power);

  const wantedActivePerks = wanted.perks?.filter(p => p.isActive) || [];
  const perkTotal = wantedActivePerks.length;
  let perkMatches = 0;
  if (wantedActivePerks.length > 0 && candidate.perks) {
    const candidateActiveHashes = new Set(candidate.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.perkHash));
    const candidateActiveNames = new Set(candidate.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map(p => p.name.toLowerCase()));
    for (const wp of wantedActivePerks) {
      if (candidateActiveHashes.has(wp.perkHash) || candidateActiveNames.has(wp.name.toLowerCase())) {
        perkMatches++;
        score += 200;
      }
    }
  }

  let modMatches = 0;
  const modTotal = wanted.mods?.length || 0;
  if (wanted.mods && wanted.mods.length > 0 && candidate.mods) {
    const candidateModHashes = new Set(candidate.mods.map(m => m.plugHash));
    const candidateModNames = new Set(candidate.mods.map(m => m.name.toLowerCase()));
    for (const wm of wanted.mods) {
      if (candidateModHashes.has(wm.plugHash) || candidateModNames.has(wm.name.toLowerCase())) {
        modMatches++;
        score += 100;
      }
    }
  }

  const statDiffs: StatDiff[] = [];
  if (isArmor && wanted.stats && wanted.stats.length > 0 && candidate.stats && candidate.stats.length > 0) {
    for (const ws of wanted.stats) {
      if (!STAT_NAMES.includes(ws.name)) continue;
      const candidateStatEntry = candidate.stats.find(s => s.statHash === ws.statHash) || candidate.stats.find(s => s.name === ws.name);
      const matchedVal = candidateStatEntry?.value || 0;
      const diff = matchedVal - ws.value;
      statDiffs.push({ name: ws.name, wanted: ws.value, matched: matchedVal, diff });
      score += Math.max(0, 30 - Math.abs(diff));
    }
  }

  return { score, perkMatches, perkTotal, modMatches, modTotal, statDiffs };
}

export default function SharePage() {
  const [, params] = useRoute('/share/:shareCode');
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const shareCode = params?.shareCode;
  const { isAuthenticated, destinyProfile, login } = useBungieAuth();
  const { t } = useI18n();

  const { data: profileState, isLoading: isLoadingProfile } = useProfileState(
    destinyProfile?.membershipType,
    destinyProfile?.membershipId,
  );

  const transferItem = useTransferItem();
  const equipItem = useEquipItem();

  const [showEquipModal, setShowEquipModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedCharId, setSelectedCharId] = useState<string>('');
  const [isEquipping, setIsEquipping] = useState(false);
  const [equipProgress, setEquipProgress] = useState('');

  const ITEM_SLOTS = [
    { id: 'kinetic', label: t('slot.kinetic'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'energy', label: t('slot.energy'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'power', label: t('slot.power'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'helmet', label: t('slot.helmet'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'arms', label: t('slot.arms'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'chest', label: t('slot.chest'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'legs', label: t('slot.legs'), icon: () => null },
    { id: 'class', label: t('slot.class'), icon: () => null },
  ];

  const { data: loadout, isLoading, error } = useQuery<SavedLoadout>({
    queryKey: [buildUrl(api.loadouts.getByShareCode.path, { shareCode: shareCode! })],
    enabled: !!shareCode,
  });

  const characters = profileState?.characters || [];

  const selectedChar = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedCharId) return characters[0] || null;
    return characters.find(c => c.characterId === selectedCharId) || characters[0] || null;
  }, [characters, selectedCharId]);

  const matchResults = useMemo<MatchResult[] | null>(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated || !profileState || !loadout || !selectedChar) return null;

    const loadoutItems = loadout.items as Record<string, Item>;
    const results: MatchResult[] = [];
    const charId = selectedChar.characterId;
    const charInventory = selectedChar.inventory || [];
    const charEquipped = selectedChar.equippedItems || [];
    const vaultItems = profileState.vaultItems || [];
    const otherChars = characters.filter(c => c.characterId !== charId);

    for (const slot of ITEM_SLOTS) {
      const wanted = loadoutItems[slot.id] as Item | undefined;
      if (!wanted) {
        results.push({ slot: slot.id, wanted: null, matched: null, status: 'exact' });
        continue;
      }

      const exactInEquipped = charEquipped.find(i => i.name === wanted.name && i.slot === wanted.slot);
      if (exactInEquipped) {
        results.push({ slot: slot.id, wanted, matched: exactInEquipped, status: 'exact', source: 'equipped' });
        continue;
      }

      const exactInInventory = charInventory.find(i => i.name === wanted.name && i.slot === wanted.slot);
      if (exactInInventory) {
        results.push({ slot: slot.id, wanted, matched: exactInInventory, status: 'exact', source: 'inventory' });
        continue;
      }

      const exactInVault = vaultItems.find(i => i.name === wanted.name && i.slot === wanted.slot);
      if (exactInVault) {
        results.push({ slot: slot.id, wanted, matched: exactInVault, status: 'exact', source: 'vault' });
        continue;
      }

      let exactInOther: InventoryItem | undefined;
      let exactOtherCharId = '';
      for (const oc of otherChars) {
        const found = [...(oc.equippedItems || []), ...(oc.inventory || [])].find(i => i.name === wanted.name && i.slot === wanted.slot);
        if (found) {
          exactInOther = { ...found, characterId: found.characterId || oc.characterId };
          exactOtherCharId = oc.characterId;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (exactInOther) {
        results.push({ slot: slot.id, wanted, matched: exactInOther, status: 'exact', source: 'other_character', sourceCharId: exactOtherCharId });
        continue;
      }

      const allAvailable: { item: InventoryItem; src: ItemSource; srcCharId?: string }[] = [
        ...charInventory.filter(i => i.slot === wanted.slot).map(i => ({ item: i, src: 'inventory' as ItemSource })),
        ...vaultItems.filter(i => i.slot === wanted.slot).map(i => ({ item: i, src: 'vault' as ItemSource })),
      ];
      for (const oc of otherChars) {
        const ocItems = [...(oc.equippedItems || []), ...(oc.inventory || [])].filter(i => i.slot === wanted.slot);
        for (const oi of ocItems) {
          allAvailable.push({ item: { ...oi, characterId: oi.characterId || oc.characterId }, src: 'other_character', srcCharId: oc.characterId });
        }
      }

      const isArmorSlot = ARMOR_SLOTS.includes(slot.id);
      const scored = allAvailable.map(entry => {
        const result = scoreCandidateMatch(wanted, entry.item, isArmorSlot);
        return { ...entry, ...result };
      }).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

      const best = scored[0];

      if (best) {
        const powerDiff = ARMOR_SLOTS.includes(slot.id) ? best.item.power - (wanted.power || 0) : undefined;
        let substituteReason: string | undefined;
        if (best.item.name === wanted.name) {
          substituteReason = best.item.itemHash !== wanted.itemHash
            ? `Same weapon, different variant`
            : undefined;
        } else if (best.item.type === wanted.type) {
          substituteReason = `${wanted.name} not in inventory — best ${wanted.type} match`;
        } else {
          substituteReason = `${wanted.name} not found — closest match by slot`;
        }
        results.push({
          slot: slot.id, wanted, matched: best.item, status: 'substitute', source: best.src, sourceCharId: best.srcCharId,
          note: `${best.item.name} (${best.item.power})`, powerDiff,
          statDiffs: best.statDiffs.length > 0 ? best.statDiffs : undefined,
          perkMatchCount: best.perkMatches,
          perkTotal: best.perkTotal,
          matchScore: best.score,
          substituteReason,
        });
      } else {
        results.push({ slot: slot.id, wanted, matched: null, status: 'missing', note: wanted.name });
      }
    }

    return results;
  }, [isAuthenticated, profileState, loadout, selectedChar, characters]);

  const powerSummary = useMemo(() => {
    if (!matchResults) return null;

    let totalPowerDiff = 0;
    let substitutions = 0;
    let missing = 0;

    for (const r of matchResults) {
      if (!r.wanted) continue;
      if (r.status === 'substitute') {
        substitutions++;
        if (r.powerDiff !== undefined) totalPowerDiff += r.powerDiff;
      }
      if (r.status === 'missing') missing++;
    }

    if (substitutions === 0 && missing === 0) return null;
    return { totalPowerDiff, substitutions, missing };
  }, [matchResults]);

  const needsTransfers = useMemo(() => {
    if (!matchResults) return [];
    return matchResults.filter(r => r.matched && (r.source === 'vault' || r.source === 'other_character'));
  }, [matchResults]);

  const handleEquip = async () => {
    if (!matchResults || !selectedChar || !destinyProfile) return;
    setIsEquipping(true);

    try {
      for (const r of needsTransfers) {
        if (!r.matched) continue;
        setEquipProgress(`${t('share.transferRequired')}: ${r.matched.name}`);

        if (r.source === 'other_character') {
          const srcChar = r.sourceCharId || r.matched.characterId || selectedChar.characterId;
          await transferItem.mutateAsync({
            itemReferenceHash: r.matched.itemHash,
            itemId: r.matched.id,
            characterId: srcChar,
            membershipType: destinyProfile.membershipType,
            transferToVault: true,
          });
          await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 300));
        }

        if (r.source === 'vault' || r.source === 'other_character') {
          await transferItem.mutateAsync({
            itemReferenceHash: r.matched.itemHash,
            itemId: r.matched.id,
            characterId: selectedChar.characterId,
            membershipType: destinyProfile.membershipType,
            transferToVault: false,
          });
          await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 300));
        }
      }

      const toEquip = matchResults.filter(r => r.matched && r.source !== 'equipped');
      for (const r of toEquip) {
        if (!r.matched) continue;
        setEquipProgress(`${r.matched.name}...`);
        await equipItem.mutateAsync({
          itemId: r.matched.id,
          characterId: selectedChar.characterId,
          membershipType: destinyProfile.membershipType,
        });
        await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 250));
      }

      setShowEquipModal(false);
      setEquipProgress('');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setEquipProgress(t('share.equipError'));
    } finally {
      setIsEquipping(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#080b14] flex items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-indigo-500" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !loadout) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#080b14] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-display font-light text-white mb-4 tracking-widest">{t('share.notFound')}</h1>
        <button onClick={() => setLocation('/')} className="text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2 uppercase tracking-widest text-xs">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> {t('action.backToOrbit')}
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const loadoutItems = loadout.items as Record<string, Item>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#080b14] text-gray-300 p-4 md:p-6 flex flex-col items-center relative overflow-hidden">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_50%_50%,_rgba(40,40,60,0.3)_0%,_rgba(8,11,20,1)_100%)] z-0" />

      <div className="max-w-2xl w-full z-10 space-y-6">
        <div className="text-center space-y-4 pt-4">
          <Hexagon className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-white opacity-80" strokeWidth={1} />
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-display font-light text-white tracking-destiny-widest uppercase" data-testid="text-loadout-name">{loadout.name}</h1>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-indigo-400 tracking-destiny-widest font-mono uppercase opacity-70">{t('share.config')}</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#0A0A0C]/60 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/10 p-5 md:p-8 destiny-clip-sm space-y-4 shadow-2xl">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" />
            <h3 className="text-xs font-display font-light tracking-destiny-wide text-white">{t('section.equipment')}</h3>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-2">
            {ITEM_SLOTS.map(slot => {
              const item = loadoutItems[slot.id];
              if (!item) return null;
              const isArmor = ARMOR_SLOTS.includes(slot.id);
              return (
                <div key={slot.id} className="bg-black/20 border border-white/[0.06] rounded p-2.5 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-9 h-9 shrink-0 border border-white/10 overflow-hidden bg-black rounded-sm">
                      <img src={bungieIcon(item.icon)} alt={item.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-white font-medium truncate">{item.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 capitalize">{slot.label} · {item.power > 0 ? `${item.power} PL` : item.rarity}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {item.perks && item.perks.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 ml-12">
                      {item.perks.filter((p: any) => p.isActive).map((p: any, pi: number) => (
                        <div key={pi} className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-white/[0.08] bg-white/[0.02] text-gray-400" title={p.name}>
                          {p.icon && <img src={bungieIcon(p.icon)} alt="" className="w-3 h-3" />}
                          <span className="truncate max-w-[80px]">{p.name}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {isArmor && item.stats && item.stats.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 ml-12">
                      {item.stats.filter((s: any) => STAT_NAMES.includes(s.name)).map((s: any) => (
                        <span key={s.name} className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 font-mono">
                          {s.name.slice(0, 3)}: {s.value}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {isAuthenticated ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {characters.length > 1 && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-widest shrink-0">{t('share.selectChar')}</span>
                <div className="flex gap-2 flex-1">
                  {characters.map(c => (
                    <button
                      key={c.characterId}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedCharId(c.characterId)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 border text-[11px] tracking-wider uppercase transition-all ${
                        (selectedCharId || characters[0]?.characterId) === c.characterId
                          ? 'border-indigo-500/50 bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-300'
                          : 'border-white/10 bg-white/[0.02] text-gray-500 hover:border-white/20'
                      }`}
                      data-testid={`button-select-char-${c.characterId}`}
                    >
                      {c.emblemPath && <img src={bungieIcon(c.emblemPath)} alt="" className="w-4 h-4 rounded" />}
                      <span>{c.className}</span>
                      <span className="text-[11px] opacity-60">{c.light}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {isLoadingProfile ? (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-4 text-gray-500">
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                <span className="text-xs">{t('share.searchingInventory')}</span>
              </div>
            ) : matchResults ? (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="bg-[#0A0A0C]/40 border border-white/[0.06] p-4 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                    <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest">{t('share.statChanges')}</span>
                  </div>
                  {matchResults.filter(r => r.wanted).map(r => {
                    const statusColor = r.status === 'exact' ? 'text-green-400' : r.status === 'substitute' ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-red-400';
                    const statusBg = r.status === 'exact' ? 'bg-green-500/5 border-green-500/15' : r.status === 'substitute' ? 'bg-amber-500/5 border-amber-500/15' : 'bg-red-500/5 border-red-500/15';
                    return (
                      <div key={r.slot} className={`p-2.5 border rounded ${statusBg} space-y-1.5`}>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <div className="w-8 h-8 shrink-0 border border-white/10 overflow-hidden bg-black rounded-sm">
                            {(r.matched || r.wanted) && (
                              <img src={bungieIcon((r.matched || r.wanted)!.icon)} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            <p className={`text-[11px] ${statusColor} truncate font-medium`}>
                              {r.status === 'exact' && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"><Check className="w-3 h-3 inline" /> {r.wanted!.name}</span>}
                              {r.status === 'substitute' && r.matched!.name}
                              {r.status === 'missing' && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"><AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3 inline" /> {r.wanted!.name}</span>}
                            </p>
                            {r.status === 'substitute' && (
                              <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-400/60">
                                {r.substituteReason || t('share.substitute')} · {r.wanted!.name}
                                {r.powerDiff !== undefined && r.powerDiff !== 0 && (
                                  <span className={`ml-1 ${r.powerDiff > 0 ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'}`}>
                                    ({r.powerDiff > 0 ? '+' : ''}{r.powerDiff} PL)
                                  </span>
                                )}
                                {r.perkTotal !== undefined && r.perkTotal > 0 && (
                                  <span className={`ml-1 ${r.perkMatchCount === r.perkTotal ? 'text-green-400' : r.perkMatchCount && r.perkMatchCount > 0 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-red-400'}`}>
                                    · {r.perkMatchCount || 0}/{r.perkTotal} perks
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                            {r.source === 'vault' && (
                              <span className="text-[11px] text-indigo-400/70 bg-indigo-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t('share.fromVault')}</span>
                            )}
                            {r.source === 'other_character' && (
                              <span className="text-[11px] text-purple-400/70 bg-purple-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t('share.fromChar')}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {r.wanted?.perks && r.wanted.perks.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 ml-11">
                            {r.wanted.perks.filter(p => p.isActive).map((p, pi) => {
                              const candidateActivePerks = r.matched?.perks?.filter(mp => mp.isActive) || [];
                              const matched = r.status === 'exact' || candidateActivePerks.some(mp => mp.perkHash === p.perkHash || mp.name.toLowerCase() === p.name.toLowerCase());
                              return (
                                <div key={pi} className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border ${matched ? 'border-green-500/20 bg-green-500/5 text-green-400/80' : 'border-red-500/15 bg-red-500/5 text-red-400/60'}`} title={p.name}>
                                  {p.icon && <img src={bungieIcon(p.icon)} alt="" className="w-3 h-3" />}
                                  <span className="truncate max-w-[80px]">{p.name}</span>
                                </div>
                              );
                            })}
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {r.statDiffs && r.statDiffs.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 ml-11">
                            {r.statDiffs.map(sd => (
                              <span key={sd.name} className={`text-[11px] font-mono ${sd.diff > 0 ? 'text-green-400/70' : sd.diff < 0 ? 'text-red-400/70' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
                                {sd.name.slice(0, 3)}: {sd.matched} {sd.diff !== 0 && `(${sd.diff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${sd.diff})`}
                              </span>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                {powerSummary && (
                  <div className="bg-[#0A0A0C]/40 border border-white/[0.06] p-3 rounded flex items-center justify-between" data-testid="panel-power-summary">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--color-gold-accent)]" />
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest">{t('share.totalImpact')}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      {powerSummary.totalPowerDiff !== 0 && (
                        <span className={`text-xs font-mono font-medium ${powerSummary.totalPowerDiff > 0 ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'}`}>
                          {powerSummary.totalPowerDiff > 0 ? '+' : ''}{powerSummary.totalPowerDiff} PL
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-amber-400/80">{powerSummary.substitutions} {t('share.substitute').toLowerCase()}</span>
                      {powerSummary.missing > 0 && (
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-red-400/80">{powerSummary.missing} {t('share.missingItem').toLowerCase()}</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowEquipModal(true)}
                  className="w-full py-4 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white transition-all destiny-clip-sm text-[11px] tracking-destiny-widest font-bold uppercase flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  data-testid="button-equip-loadout"
                >
                  <Zap className="w-4 h-4" />
                  {t('share.equipNow')}
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <button
              onClick={login}
              className="w-full py-4 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white transition-all destiny-clip-sm text-[11px] tracking-destiny-widest font-bold uppercase"
            >
              {t('action.loginToAdopt')}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setLocation('/')}
              className="w-full py-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 hover:border-white/40 text-white transition-all destiny-clip-sm text-[11px] tracking-destiny-widest font-bold uppercase"
            >
              {t('action.createOwn')}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {showEquipModal && matchResults && (
          <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
            onClick={() => !isEquipping && setShowEquipModal(false)}>
            <motion.div initial={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }} exit={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }}
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              className="bg-[#141417] border border-white/10 p-6 w-full max-w-lg shadow-2xl max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto destiny-clip-sm">

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-base font-display font-light text-white tracking-wider">{t('share.equipTitle')}</h2>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-1">{t('share.equipDesc')}</p>
                </div>
                {!isEquipping && (
                  <button onClick={() => setShowEquipModal(false)} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white">
                    <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              {selectedChar && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 p-2 bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded">
                  {selectedChar.emblemPath && (
                    <img src={bungieIcon(selectedChar.emblemPath)} alt="" className="w-8 h-8 rounded" />
                  )}
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-white font-medium">{selectedChar.className}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{selectedChar.light} · {selectedChar.raceName}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="space-y-2 mb-4">
                {matchResults.filter(r => r.wanted).map(result => {
                  const statusIcon = result.status === 'exact' ? <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-green-400" /> :
                    result.status === 'substitute' ? <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" /> :
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3 text-red-400" />;
                  const borderClass = result.status === 'exact' ? 'border-green-500/15' :
                    result.status === 'substitute' ? 'border-amber-500/15' : 'border-red-500/15';

                  return (
                    <div key={result.slot} className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-2 border ${borderClass} rounded bg-black/20`}>
                      {statusIcon}
                      <div className="w-6 h-6 shrink-0 border border-white/10 overflow-hidden bg-black rounded-sm">
                        {(result.matched || result.wanted) && (
                          <img src={bungieIcon((result.matched || result.wanted)!.icon)} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-white truncate">
                          {result.status === 'exact' ? result.wanted!.name : result.status === 'substitute' ? result.matched!.name : result.wanted!.name}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 capitalize">{result.slot}</p>
                      </div>
                      {result.source === 'vault' && <span className="text-[11px] text-indigo-400/70 bg-indigo-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t('share.fromVault')}</span>}
                      {result.source === 'other_character' && <span className="text-[11px] text-purple-400/70 bg-purple-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t('share.fromChar')}</span>}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {needsTransfers.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4 p-2 bg-indigo-500/5 border border-indigo-500/15 rounded">
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-indigo-400">
                    {t('share.transferRequired')} · {needsTransfers.length} {t('ingame.items').toLowerCase()}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {isEquipping && equipProgress && (
                <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 p-2 bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] rounded">
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-indigo-400 shrink-0" />
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 truncate">{equipProgress}</p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowEquipModal(false)}
                  disabled={isEquipping}
                  className="flex-1 py-3 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-white text-[11px] tracking-destiny-wide uppercase font-bold hover:bg-white/10 transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {t('action.cancel')}
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={handleEquip}
                  disabled={isEquipping}
                  className="flex-1 py-3 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white text-[11px] tracking-destiny-wide uppercase font-bold transition-all disabled:opacity-70 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  data-testid="button-confirm-equip"
                >
                  {isEquipping ? (
                    <><Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> {t('share.equipping')}</>
                  ) : (
                    <><Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {t('share.confirm')}</>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Link } from 'wouter';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { appleEase } from '@/components/dashboard/shared';

function Section({ title, children, delay = 0 }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode; delay?: number }) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 16 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay, duration: 0.5, ease: appleEase }}
      className="mb-8"
    >
      <h2 className="font-display text-sm tracking-destiny-wide uppercase text-white mb-3">{title}</h2>
      <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 leading-relaxed tracking-wide whitespace-pre-line">{children}</div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export function ImprintPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[100dvh] bg-[#080b14] text-gray-300" style={{ overscrollBehavior: 'none' }}>
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-6 py-12">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }}
        >
          <Link href="/" data-testid="link-back-home">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] tracking-destiny-wide uppercase text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer mb-8">
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {t('legal.backToHome')}
            </span>
          </Link>
        </motion.div>

        <motion.h1
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.1, duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase }}
          className="font-display text-2xl tracking-destiny uppercase text-white mt-6 mb-8"
          data-testid="text-imprint-title"
        >
          {t('imprint.title')}
        </motion.h1>

        <Section title={t('imprint.info')} delay={0.2}>
          <p className="font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">{t('imprint.name')}</p>
          <p>{t('imprint.address')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('imprint.contact')} delay={0.3}>
          <p>{t('imprint.email')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('imprint.responsibility')} delay={0.35}>
          <p>{t('imprint.responsibilityText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('imprint.authority')} delay={0.4}>
          <p>{t('imprint.authorityText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('imprint.dispute')} delay={0.45}>
          <p>{t('imprint.disputeText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <LegalFooter />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[100dvh] bg-[#080b14] text-gray-300" style={{ overscrollBehavior: 'none' }}>
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-6 py-12">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: appleEase }}
        >
          <Link href="/" data-testid="link-back-home-privacy">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] tracking-destiny-wide uppercase text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer mb-8">
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {t('legal.backToHome')}
            </span>
          </Link>
        </motion.div>

        <motion.h1
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.1, duration: 0.6, ease: appleEase }}
          className="font-display text-2xl tracking-destiny uppercase text-white mt-6 mb-4"
          data-testid="text-privacy-title"
        >
          {t('privacy.title')}
        </motion.h1>

        <motion.p
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          transition={{ delay: 0.15, duration: 0.5 }}
          className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 leading-relaxed tracking-wide mb-10"
        >
          {t('privacy.intro')}
        </motion.p>

        <Section title={t('privacy.responsibleTitle')} delay={0.2}>
          <p>{t('privacy.responsibleText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('privacy.dataTitle')} delay={0.25}>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">{t('privacy.dataAuth')}</p>
              <p>{t('privacy.dataAuthText')}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">{t('privacy.dataProfile')}</p>
              <p>{t('privacy.dataProfileText')}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">{t('privacy.dataLoadouts')}</p>
              <p>{t('privacy.dataLoadoutsText')}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">{t('privacy.dataSessions')}</p>
              <p>{t('privacy.dataSessionsText')}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('privacy.cookiesTitle')} delay={0.3}>
          <p>{t('privacy.cookiesText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('privacy.aiTitle')} delay={0.35}>
          <p>{t('privacy.aiText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('privacy.thirdPartyTitle')} delay={0.4}>
          <ul className="space-y-1.5 list-disc list-inside">
            <li>{t('privacy.thirdPartyBungie')}</li>
            <li>{t('privacy.thirdPartyOpenAI')}</li>
            <li>{t('privacy.thirdPartyCF')}</li>
          </ul>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('privacy.rightsTitle')} delay={0.45}>
          <p>{t('privacy.rightsText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <Section title={t('privacy.contactTitle')} delay={0.5}>
          <p>{t('privacy.contactText')}</p>
        </Section>

        <LegalFooter />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function LegalFooter() {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <div className="mt-12 pt-6 border-t border-white/[0.06] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4">
      <Link href="/imprint" data-testid="link-imprint">
        <span className="text-[11px] tracking-destiny-wide uppercase text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
          {t('footer.imprint')}
        </span>
      </Link>
      <span className="text-gray-700 text-[11px]">|</span>
      <Link href="/privacy" data-testid="link-privacy">
        <span className="text-[11px] tracking-destiny-wide uppercase text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
          {t('footer.privacy')}
        </span>
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: client/src/pages/not-found.tsx
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";

export default function NotFound() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-50">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-md mx-4">
        <CardContent className="pt-6">
          <div className="flex mb-4 gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="h-8 w-8 text-red-500" />
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">404 Page Not Found</h1>
          </div>

          <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-600">
            Did you forget to add the page to the router?
          </p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
